cua-driver-rs-v0.47.19cua-driver-rs: v0.47.19 Prerelease2026-11-12T11:45:58Z

Cua Driver 0.47.19

Summary

This release includes 1 fix.

Fixes

  • load libxkbcommon's 1.0 text functions at run time so the driver starts on Ubuntu 20.04. (#6498) Thanks @factory-ain3sh, @injaneity.

Contributors

Thanks to @factory-ain3sh, @injaneity for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

cua-hyprland-kit-v1.4.0-omarchy-edge-20261111Hyprland build kit v1.4.0 for Omarchy Edge 20261111 Stable2026-11-11T18:55:25Z

Hyprland build kit for Omarchy Edge x86_64 (Hyprland 0.57.2)

This kit rebuilds the optional Cua Hyprland plugin from Driver 0.47.8 against the omarchy-edge-20261111 profile (Hyprland 0.57.2-1). It contains source, packaging tools, checksums and provenance. It is not a prebuilt or Omarchy-signed plugin package.

  • Source: 87558bfd064787c7839351c55d667dc8244044a4, the cua-driver-rs-v0.47.8 tag.
  • Tooling: built with profile_bundle.py at 9e7256bfbb5ded8dc800484e43846b2df938af6d, the merge commit of #7390.
  • Evidence: CI in #7390 builds the package, runs the install, remove and reinstall lifecycle, loads the packaged module into a headless Hyprland on 0.57.2-1 (edge) and 0.56.2-4 (stable), and passes the background type and click smoke with the released Driver. This is CI evidence, not native certification or Omabot replay.

Verify the archive against its published SHA-256 (the .sha256 asset) and review PROFILE.json and KIT-PROVENANCE.json before building.

Omarchy owns Omabot replay, package maintenance and signing, and channel promotion. This release does not put a package into Omarchy's repositories.

cua-driver-rs-v0.47.11cua-driver-rs: v0.47.11 Prerelease2026-11-11T17:41:28Z

Cua Driver 0.47.11

Summary

This release includes 1 fix.

Fixes

  • stamp the window-local point on macOS 27 background drags. (#7372)

Contributors

This release contains maintainer changes only.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

<!-- macos-install-note -->

macOS install note

The app isn't signed with an Apple Developer ID yet, so macOS may call a downloaded build "damaged" — it's Gatekeeper quarantine, not a bad file. After moving the app to Applications, clear the flag once, then open normally:

xattr -dr com.apple.quarantine "/Applications/OpenHands Agent Canvas.app"

Signing + notarization is tracked in OpenHands/OpenHands#16774.

heygen-com/hyperframes ↗ 6 releases · 6 stable
v0.9.39 Stable2026-11-12T13:55:31Z

HyperFrames v0.9.39

Released on 2026-11-12.

Studio shows the move, resize and rotate readout in a chip under the element. Desktop and other hosts can now import key parsing and defaults from a light @hyperframes/studio/keys path.

Features

  • Studio: Show the move, resize and rotate readout as a chip under the element (6e0e03f54, #7588).
  • Studio: Add a light @hyperframes/studio/keys subpath for key parsing and defaults (234fb1aee, #7600).
  • Studio: Picker styles and composition size read an opaque preview over its channel (01618d504, #7602).
  • Studio: Playback and timeline reads go over the preview's message channel (940963afa, #7561).

Fixes

  • Ci: A release fails its checks when its notes miss a commit merged after the cut (5d2914f7c, #7601).

v0.9.38 Stable2026-11-12T08:55:17Z

HyperFrames v0.9.38

Released on 2026-11-12.

The Volume control in Studio now changes the whole clip, a paused video shows the same colors as the playing one on Mac, and edit history records which layer each edit touched. v0.9.37 Stable2026-11-12T02:30:31Z

HyperFrames v0.9.37

Released on 2026-11-12.

Sharing refuses to build an archive when it can't read .hyperframesignore, so ignored files stay out. templates pull also refuses archives that hold links.

Fixes

  • CLI: hyperframes templates pull refuses an archive that holds anything other than plain files and folders, such as a symlink, hard link or device entry. Nothing is unpacked, and no partial folder is left behind. A link inside a pulled template could otherwise let preview, render and publish reach files elsewhere on your machine (ab66e5bd7, #7576).
  • Share: snapshotProject and snapshotFiles throw invalid_source_ignore again when .hyperframesignore can't be read. In 0.9.35 they skipped the unreadable file, so an archive built from the snapshot could include files the ignore file meant to leave out. Hosts that use these functions only for change detection should pass { lenientIgnore: true } to keep the 0.9.35 behavior. projectFingerprint already does (35cf03cab, #7573).

HyperFrames v0.9.36

Released on 2026-11-11.

Motion blur previews more smoothly when you lower preview quality. Renders still draw full-quality blur.

Fixes

  • Lint: Don't look for audio on another computer's share (7ba8f8507, #7572).

v0.9.35 Stable2026-11-11T22:38:48Z

HyperFrames v0.9.35

Released on 2026-11-11.

Private files like .env and git data stay out of preview bundles, and local sharing only answers your own machine. Sharing also keeps tracking changes when .hyperframesignore uses gitignore patterns.

Fixes

  • Security: Preview bundles never include .env files or git data, even through a link. A render that names one of these files now stops with an error. Local sharing only answers this machine, so sharing from Studio in a container now gets a 403 (a243169f8, #7563).
  • Security: Helper programs are found by walking your trusted PATH folders directly. On Windows, the working folder is never searched (9a048ac01, #7562).
  • Share: Change detection keeps working when .hyperframesignore uses gitignore patterns such as /exports/ or **/*.mov. Before, those patterns stopped it with an error (c07468d6a, #7568).
  • CLI: Durations and sizes roll over to the next unit when rounded, so you see 1m 0.0s and 1.0 MB, not 60.0s and 1024.0 KB (182608311, #7565).

Docs & Examples

  • Studio App: The brand kits page says kits are shared with your workspace only (c218b37a3, #7570).

Internal

  • Core: The preview runtime offers its parent a typed message port. This prepares Studio for a sealed preview frame and changes nothing yet (ef5cdeb0a, #7556).
  • CLI: Local Studio's share window counts button clicks and choices. Only fixed categories are recorded, never links, paths or email addresses (2a70733c1, #7566).

HyperFrames v0.9.34

Released on 2026-11-11.

HyperFrames now runs helper programs like FFmpeg only from where they are installed, never from a project folder. Renders can also refuse variables a page does not declare, when that check is turned on.

Features

  • Producer: With strict variables turned on, a render fails when it is given a variable the page does not declare. It is off by default, so renders are unchanged (f312fca7e, #7548).

Fixes

  • Studio Server: Load fonts and modules only in the Studio's own preview frames (ffb13c3b6, #7553).

  • CLI: Preview uses the bundled Studio unless local Studio mode is opted in (668983e1a, #7551).
  • Security: FFmpeg, FFprobe and other helper programs are taken only from where they are installed or a path you set, never from a project folder (84799bdc5, #7524).
  • Core: Saving a file works on drives that refuse hard links with a permission error (f36463e29, #7474).
  • Studio: Thumbnails are captured in a sealed page, and shader transitions still work where the browser blocks local storage (80cda85d7, #7528).

  • CLI: Refuse more coding-agent instruction files in registry installs (9bab57ab4, #7549).
  • CLI and Studio: Registry installs and Studio file writes stay inside the project content folders (b96c82b78, #7523).

Internal

  • Engine: The URL-read concurrency test passes on machines with 32 or more cores (3ad7611f9, #7544).

v0.9.1Skill Recorder v0.9.1 Stable2026-11-12T13:20:43Z

Commit-pinned installation

Copy the complete command for your operating system and paste it into PowerShell (Windows) or Terminal (macOS/Ubuntu). Use the same command to install or update.

Windows 11 x64 or ARM64

$commit="21bcc5ea48e8c8aa8730e8272bff3d1b1bab9e4d"; $env:SKILL_RECORDER_COMMIT=$commit; irm "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/microsoft/skill-recorder/$commit/install.ps1" | iex

macOS or Ubuntu

commit="21bcc5ea48e8c8aa8730e8272bff3d1b1bab9e4d"; curl -fsSL "https://raw.githubusercontent.com/microsoft/skill-recorder/$commit/install.sh" | SKILL_RECORDER_COMMIT="$commit" bash

For script inspection and hash verification before execution, use the inspect-first instructions.

Fix

Keep using an existing GitHub login after upgrading. If no AI connection preference has been saved yet, Skill Recorder now checks the existing Copilot CLI authentication and automatically selects GitHub when already signed in. Users upgrading from 0.8.0 no longer need to choose their connection again just because the new BYOM setting was introduced.

  • Existing credentials are reused in their original location; they are not copied, deleted, or replaced.
  • Saved GitHub or BYOM choices remain authoritative. Forgetting a BYOM key does not silently switch the user back to GitHub.
  • Users without an existing login still see the GitHub/BYOM chooser. Both options remain available through AI connection in Sessions.
  • The migration check is bounded, runs once per app lifetime, and is coordinated with explicit setup and shutdown. A failed check displays a safe warning and leaves manual setup available.

Upgrade and compatibility

  • Re-run the commit-pinned source installer above to update.
  • This patch does not recover deleted or expired credentials. If the existing GitHub login cannot be verified, choose GitHub authentication to retry.
  • Dependencies and installer scripts are unchanged. BYOM provider setup, key storage, privacy protections, and billing behavior remain as documented in v0.9.0.
  • Locally assembled source builds are not redistributable release binaries.

Source-only release

Skill Recorder 0.9.1 is published from commit 21bcc5ea48e8c8aa8730e8272bff3d1b1bab9e4d.

This release is source-only. No installers or portable binaries are attached. GitHub's generated archives contain the tagged repository source only.

Validation

  • 298 local unit/regression/import tests passed, including upgrade restoration, existing provider preferences, failure handling, concurrent setup, and shutdown.
  • The actual bundled SDK/CLI BYOM image/tool transport and credential-persistence fixture passed without GitHub authentication.
  • Production build, application/eval type checks, lockfile portability, and a 285-package license inventory passed.
  • Exact release-commit CI passed on Windows x64/ARM64 and macOS/Ubuntu, covering commit-pinned source installation, license/compliance checks, tests, builds, BYOM runtime transport, and applicable packaging/architecture checks.

Installer script SHA-256

v0.9.0Skill Recorder v0.9.0 Stable2026-11-12T12:12:37Z

Source-only release

Skill Recorder 0.9.0 is published from commit 53447f6cca45ad84468ff8898b182c37fb7ab9e5.

This release is source-only. No installers or portable binaries are attached. GitHub's generated archives contain the tagged repository source only.

Changes

  • Choose how to power AI: at the first Analyze/Create action, select Bring your own model or GitHub authentication. Recording remains available without either. The choice is available to everyone, including Microsoft employees.
  • Bring your own model: use Microsoft Foundry/Azure OpenAI, OpenAI, Anthropic, or a custom OpenAI-compatible provider without GitHub login or a Copilot subscription. Provider billing applies.
  • Configure your API key, endpoint, and model/deployment name, with API-format and Azure-version options where applicable. Standard OpenAI/Anthropic URLs are prefilled. The selected model must support images and tool calling.
  • Test and save verifies image/tool support using a small, provider-billed synthetic test, not a recording.
  • Remember keys securely: use OS-protected storage when available. If it is unavailable, explain this on the key-entry screen and continue automatically using memory only, without an extra step or blocking BYOM.
  • Forget key removes this app's stored and in-memory credential. Change connections later through AI connection in Sessions.
  • Preserve the existing Microsoft employee Enterprise SSO and personal GitHub sign-in flows under GitHub authentication.
  • Apply the selected provider consistently to analysis, skill generation, and automation generation. Preserve tool restrictions and cancellation/retry safeguards, and reset live AI conversations when a connection changes.
  • Show provider-aware data-destination disclosures and isolate BYOM sessions from unrelated CLI configuration.

Compatibility, privacy, and upgrade notes

  • BYOM is not automatically local/offline or free. Requests go to the configured provider, under its availability, rate limits, policies, and billing.
  • A provider's model catalog does not guarantee every model can analyze recordings. Image input and tool calling are required; custom endpoints must implement a compatible API.
  • Foundry v1 uses a full base URL ending in /openai/v1/; native versioned Azure uses a resource host and API version. The model field is the deployment name for Azure, which may differ from the base model name.
  • Keyless local-service onboarding and managed-identity authentication are not exposed by this release.
  • Windows uses DPAPI, macOS uses Keychain, and Ubuntu needs an available secure desktop keyring to remember keys. Without secure storage, the key is kept only until the app closes and is requested again on the next launch.
  • Forgetting a key does not revoke it at the provider or erase recordings and SDK conversation history. Connection state lives outside recording folders, and credentials are excluded from debug exports.
  • Finish or cancel active AI work before changing its connection. Saved recordings and generated outputs remain intact.
  • Existing local transcription and sensitive-data protection remain in place. Review inferred activities and generated instructions; protection is a safety net, not a guarantee.
  • Dependency versions and installer scripts are unchanged. Re-run the commit-pinned source installer below to update. Locally assembled builds are not redistributable release binaries.

Validation

  • Local validation: 291 unit/regression/import tests, production build, application/eval type checks, lockfile portability, and a 285-package license inventory.
  • The actual bundled SDK/CLI completed a synthetic image-and-tool turn against a local provider fixture without GitHub authentication; runtime artifacts were checked for provider-key persistence.
  • An isolated UI preview covered initial selection, the memory-only disclaimer, connection save/analysis continuation, cancellation, and forgetting.
  • Native Windows ARM64 protected-key persistence, restart recovery, and removal were exercised.
  • PR CI passed on Windows x64/ARM64, macOS, and Ubuntu. The initial ARM64 compliance test process failed after all assertions passed; a targeted rerun passed without code changes.
  • Exact release-commit CI passed on Windows x64/ARM64 and macOS/Ubuntu, covering commit-pinned source installation, license/compliance checks, tests, builds, BYOM runtime transport, and applicable packaging/architecture checks.
  • Native secure-storage smoke checks on macOS, Ubuntu, and Windows x64, and paid live-provider checks, have not been performed in this session. Automated transport tests do not establish native storage or live-provider behavior.

Installer script SHA-256

These hashes were calculated from the exact release commit's Git blobs.

"Magic is a world of visual imagination." — Frieren, 《Frieren: Beyond Journey's End》 (2023)

✨ What's New

Feature Description By
Create mode Say in one sentence how you want a site to change; the site's AI writes a small plugin, and Voyager reviews it with you and installs it. Open the panel with Alt/⌥+V and pick Create. Docs —
Recolor Voyager A creation can recolor Voyager's own panels, menus and timeline through public --gv-theme-* variables. The built-in Nord palette (off by default) was made this way: its first version was written by Gemini (Argon) in Create mode. (closes #821) —
Creations that run code (off by default) For what a style change cannot do, the AI can write a script. You see the full source first and turn on Chrome's user scripts switch yourself. Desktop Chrome and Edge only. —
Show the AI the page A request can carry the structure of the current page, with none of its text, previewed before sending. Gemini warns when the selected model usually cannot write a plugin. —
Fix it when it goes wrong A reply that cannot install says why, an installed creation that changes nothing is flagged, and both offer Ask it to fix. —
No refresh after an update Open tabs pick up the new version on their own; a tab that cannot shows a card and reloads only on a click. —
Update reminders Choose between opening the release notes, a NEW mark on the floating ball, or nothing; notes for skipped versions are shown together. —
Font size in 1% steps Reading font size takes a typed number and resets to 100%. PR #1086 by @eacheat53

🐛 Bug Fixes

Fix Description By
ChatGPT export Very long conversations export in full, and the export button no longer jumps while the page loads. (closes #1071) —
ChatGPT timeline Each run in a scheduled task conversation gets its own marker. (closes #1091) —
ChatGPT Vim mode The mode badge stays beside the composer, on Codex too. —
Gemini sidebar With auto-hide on, opening the account switcher no longer collapses the sidebar. —
Theme Until a theme is picked, Voyager follows the system's light or dark. PR #1085 by @eacheat53
Touch A finger can drag the floating ball and reach Hide. (closes #1089) —
Update reminders Badge mode marks the next release as it should. —

  • 🔔 系统通知 (Notifications): Gemini 回复完成后可发送 macOS 通知，并直达对应对话。 macOS can notify you when a Gemini response finishes and open the matching conversation.
  • 🔄 自动更新 (Automatic updates): 完成这次迁移后，后续版本可通过 Sparkle 自动更新。 After this one-time migration, future versions can update automatically through Sparkle.

@upstash/context7-mcp@4.7.0 Stable2026-11-12T09:03:12Z

Minor Changes

  • d451493: Add a page argument to the get-upgrade-guide research preview tool: a long guide now comes in pages, each in full detail. Page 1 starts with everything needed to migrate (summary, before you start, codemods, breaking changes, deprecations), which continues on the next pages when it is longer than one page; behavior, config and API changes follow. A guide is served only once it is complete, so its pages always fit together; until then the tool says it is being prepared and when to call again, or that it could not be prepared.

Patch Changes

  • f2daacb: get-upgrade-guide no longer tells the agent never to retry a guide that could not be prepared: the agent uses query-docs now and calls again only after the time the answer gives. Telemetry records an unavailable guide as unavailable and a page past the last as invalid, instead of not_found; neither is shown to the agent as an error.

morluto/rea ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
rea-agents-6.9.0rea-agents: v6.9.0 Stable2026-11-12T08:59:13Z

6.9.0 (2026-11-11)

Highlights

  • Build and test desktop tasks from recorded application state, including downloaded Linux apps, reusable build computers and multiple tasks per capture.
  • Inspect more shipped artifacts offline: standalone application bundles, embedded firmware, NTFS acquisitions, API contracts and V8 code-cache containers.
  • Keep native and runtime investigations grounded in retained Evidence, with paged results, explicit coverage and recoverable resource limits.

Changes

  • Analyze targets and create private runtimes on fixed local ReFS volumes as well as NTFS, preserving persistent ACL, file identity, snapshot and cleanup checks. #10086. Thanks @QianNangong.
  • Capture, export and witness desktop tasks inside a build computer; keep an agentenv computer available through MCP and prepare a build host from one command. #8954, #9041, #9045, #9049, #9199. Thanks @earakely-scale.
  • Install desktop apps from SHA-256-pinned AppImages or upstream archives, retain selected package dependencies, and isolate explicitly declared build secrets. #9215, #9112, #9225. Thanks @earakely-scale.
  • Choose seed/end checkpoints, open seed documents at launch, build several tasks from one capture, and evaluate unreadable app state through headless checks or a screen judge. #9037, #9054, #9216, #9217, issue morluto/rea#9676. Thanks @earakely-scale.
  • Read gzip and XML desktop documents and draft assertions for nested fields, attribute text, child text and member counts. #9050, #9111, #9114, #9132, #9185. Thanks @earakely-scale.
  • Inventory and extract .NET single-file, Bun standalone and Deno compile executables, with caller-selected entries and output destinations. #3787, #4031, #6998, #8527, issue morluto/rea#2352, issue morluto/rea#3490. Thanks @gensokyo-dev, @jsk1004ha, @matbrgz, @mvanhorn, @Rinkia, @Zhong-fan.
  • Read UnityFS bundles, Godot 3 PCK archives, WeChat mini-program packages, Zstandard ZIP members and classic iNES regions. #7699, #7781, #8148, #7780, #2816. Thanks @blankym, @gensokyo-dev, @ShuaixinHuang, @XiaoBinGan, @Zhong-fan.
  • Inspect and extract Intel HEX, Motorola S-record, U-Boot uImage/FIT, ESP flash, UF2 and DfuSe firmware regions while retaining their container declarations. #7136, #7234, #7771, #7498, #8228, #8230. Thanks @555aaditya, @Entrpi, @gensokyo-dev, @iamcipherdev.
  • Inventory offline NTFS MFT records and paired USN journal acquisitions; retain source names, journal-version coverage and verified extension-attribute ownership. #9911, #9914, #9957, #10006, issue morluto/rea#9867. Thanks @2SinceJuly, @gensokyo-dev, @jayykernel, @N0zoM1z0.
  • Inspect V8 code-cache headers and retained byte patterns without executing the cache; report Electron cachedData boundaries and custom URL protocol registrations. #8993, #9501, #2978. Thanks @2SinceJuly, @gensokyo-dev, @UnknownHacker1, @XiaoBinGan.
  • Inventory local OpenAPI operations and GraphQL SDL fields/references; compare static JavaScript endpoints with HAR observations. #9104, #9140, #9090, issue morluto/rea#3591. Thanks @gensokyo-dev, @Y0UW1SH.
  • Import saved Playwright v10 trace Evidence, page scenario summaries, reuse saved response values across steps, and validate browser scenarios offline. #9127, #7120, #7267, #7343, issue morluto/rea#5488, issue morluto/rea#6167. Thanks @debavelaerel, @earakely-scale, @gensokyo-dev, @jayykernel, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @z2Ace0107.
  • Replay captured environment writes, including variable member sets, sorted creates, references, toggles and subsequent reads that explain the resulting state. #7260, #7274, #7332, #7539, #7600. Thanks @earakely-scale.
  • Apply selected R8 mappings, inspect Android manifest component declarations, link static JNI candidates and trace callers of external DEX methods. #7456, #7379, #7472, #8154, issue morluto/rea#4448, issue morluto/rea#6329, issue morluto/rea#6403, issue morluto/rea#7811. Thanks @555aaditya, @coygeek, @gensokyo-dev, @harshjohiya, @jayykernel, @Rinkia, @xu91102.
  • Expose managed field defaults, project retained member views, suggest Unity managed members and retain IL2CPP v108 header/string Evidence. #8015, #7968, #5511, #9925, issue morluto/rea#7282. Thanks @379061296, @8rulerstar, @dhruvac29, @Entrpi, @gensokyo-dev, @Henglie, @kaoru0822-kitauji, @ShuaixinHuang, @XiaoBinGan, @xu91102.
  • Report ELF/PE/Mach-O hardening, per-slice Mach-O encryption, initializer/finalizer functions and ELF function ranges recovered from unwind tables. #8524, #5359, #9574, #9585, issue morluto/rea#4306. Thanks @chordpli, @coygeek, @gensokyo-dev, @mvanhorn.
  • Read Linux core-dump process/mapping notes, kernel version/configuration data and Android ELF identity notes; join Apple IPS images to admitted Mach-O slices. #8830, #9573, #9502, #9570. Thanks @2SinceJuly, @mvanhorn, @rishikeshgowdakk, @saariuslystoned.

⚠ BREAKING CHANGES

  • Settings clients must replace effect, rea_restart_required, restart_required and restartRequired with update_mode (runtime, restart, parameter) and pending_restart. Parameter-supplied limits cannot be changed by the runtime setter; restart-mode values retain their startup value until restart. #9885, be20bdfb26f7, aee1178839dd. Thanks @gensokyo-dev, @N0zoM1z0.
  • Process and desktop callers must inspect details.secondary_failures/secondaryFailures: mixed batches keep physical/provider failures primary and retain typed cancellation with its original cause. Retained descriptors can produce cleanup_incomplete; follow the returned recovery instead of treating it as ordinary cancellation. Custom adapters must consume owner-issued inspection/sampling facts and retain their cleanup scopes until release succeeds. #10141, #10113, #10109.
  • rea mcp add --command now accepts a local stdio launcher only: HTTP(S) executable URLs are rejected before settings writes, and package acquisition is removed. Configure remote transports in each client’s native settings and install/select the local launcher before registration. #10102.
  • Runtime-instance graph identities now require identity.capture_evidence_id in addition to the capture digest and runtime key. Node IDs digest that identity, so regenerate reconciliation Evidence and rebuild node-keyed lookups even when the digest and runtime key are unchanged. #10063.
  • Retained process symlinks require their stored target bytes; recapture legacy observations that only described a link without retaining its target. #10082.
  • Function dossiers must record Swift literal inventory and its omissions together. Regenerate one-sided observations through a canonical provider; both-absent unsupported observations remain valid. #10068.
  • Conformance manifest schema v2 separates exact string markers from fragments and rejects the old ambiguous strings field. Regenerate unversioned fixtures with npm run build:fixtures. #9977.
  • Unavailable native/BEAM/Go/Rust view collections return items.rows: null, coverage.status: "unavailable", coverage.total: null and coverage.exhausted: false; recorded empty collections still return rows: []. Refresh schemas, stop paging unavailable data and inspect unknowns; older saved views retain their recorded facts. #9893, #8291, issue morluto/rea#8276. Thanks @Xinglan233.
  • Desktop witnessing requires the current generated verifier: re-export old bundles using their original Evidence/task inputs. Pre-6.9 captures missing required state.baseline_non_files are rejected before state inspection, drafting or export; capture the application again to obtain contemporaneous SQLite member facts. #9898, #9470.
  • New browser captures record save: [] for request actions that save nothing, changing scenario/Evidence digests. Keep historical digests with historical captures and use freshly recorded digests for new ones. #9896.
  • Selected SQLite rows require row_offset and next_row_offset; rows.order is rowid, primary_key or unspecified. A null next offset means either no continuation or no stated order: an unordered sample can still have truncated: true. Inspect both order and truncated before concluding the sample is complete. #9331, issue morluto/rea#8234. Thanks @2SinceJuly, @555aaditya.
  • Windows entropy Evidence now uses the admitted filesystem path spelling, so fresh analysis of a differently cased caller path can change evidence_id. Invalidate or re-key cached associations against the fresh Evidence, retaining old IDs only for their original observations. #9393. Thanks @HusnainMaroof.
  • caller_input_paths values may be ordered arrays of strings as well as strings. Update strict consumers to preserve repeated selections. #9626, #9624.
  • observe_javascript_runtime adds required execution_context_id fields to scripts and contexts. context_key is an opaque capture-local lifetime identity, not a reusable protocol ID, and Evidence identities change. Refresh schemas and recapture when new lifetime associations are needed. #9471, issue morluto/rea#3382. Thanks @coygeek.
  • Refresh cached inspect_address_context schemas: unavailable facets may carry typed input or resource-constraint errors while successful sibling facets remain available. #8444, #8424, #8419.
  • analyze_function/trace_native_values require native_value_flow.limitations on available and unavailable variants. Ghidra memory effects cover explicit high-p-code LOAD/STORE operations only; empty effects do not prove no memory access. The value_flow_effect_scope profile commitment invalidates older snapshots: retain embedded historical Evidence, reopen without the snapshot and save a fresh one. #8651.
  • Ghidra byte searches require skipped_overlays; loaded overlays no longer count as not loaded, and adjacent initialized blocks are searched with explicit coverage. Reopen targets and save fresh analysis snapshots after the profile/search-policy changes; historical Evidence stays importable. #8608, #8376, #8840, issue morluto/rea#8374, issue morluto/rea#8563, issue morluto/rea#8607.
  • compare_functions adds function_match.method: "same-evidence" when both inputs authenticate to one Evidence ID and entry comparison is unavailable. Accept the new matched variant, retain unknown dimensions and do not infer complete equivalence; one observation cannot have contradictory changed dimensions or hashes. #8442, issue morluto/rea#8367.
  • Kuna analyze_function can return native_value_flow: {available: false, reason, limitations} instead of null. Check available and preserve the provider reason; null means procedure-scoped unrecorded unknown, not confirmed absence. The workflow-profile digest changes: older trace Evidence remains history but must be rerun to satisfy current replay. #8785, issue morluto/rea#8783.
  • The opt-in hot/cold MCP catalog no longer preloads inspect_analysis_view. Discover it before use or batch it with its producer; the default full catalog remains available. #9151. Thanks @gensokyo-dev.
  • batch_decompile and retained batch views now use items[].status: "unavailable" with reason: "no_pseudocode" for null pseudocode, replacing the former error/capability_unavailable item. Empty or non-text values remain status: "error" with unreadable_output. Both count toward failed; preserve item limitations and accept historical error items. #8737, issue morluto/rea#8736.
  • import_evidence_bundle/CLI evidence-import now reject snapshot-shaped JSON with invalid_request instead of evidence_integrity_mismatch. Restore it with open_binary.snapshot_path or a compatible CLI --snapshot; bundle imports require a canonical bundle from export_evidence_bundle. #8867.
  • REA-launched Chromium keeps its sandbox. If launch fails on root/container hosts, first enable host sandbox support, such as a suitable non-root user or namespace/setuid facilities. The explicit REA_BROWSER_NO_SANDBOX=true fallback disables Chromium’s sandbox and does not establish an REA isolation boundary. #8738, issue morluto/rea#8722. Thanks @chordpli, @hkeysai.
  • Fresh objdump Evidence changes provider identity from gnu.objdump/GNU objdump to {id: "objdump.command", name: "objdump command", version: null}; launch errors use the same ID and architecture-list subjects may be null. Update provider-keyed caches/readers and retain the selected executable parameters without assuming GNU semantics; historical Evidence stays readable. #8824, issue morluto/rea#8823.
  • Kuna verification policy is separate from analysis identity. Earlier snapshots and resumable directory exports are incompatible even with the same executable. Preserve old files, reopen without the snapshot and save a fresh one or choose a new export directory; the Kuna guide explains extracting/importing embedded Evidence as history. #8699.
  • inspect_analysis_view rejects mismatched native parent predicates with invalid_request at source.predicate_type: function/inventory/observation views require rea.analysis, and matching views require rea.native-function-matching. Select the original correctly typed Evidence or rerun its producer; patching an existing predicate invalidates its identity. #8451.
  • trace_feature can succeed when xrefs exist but owner lookup is unavailable. Accept references[].containing_procedure.status: "unavailable" with provider/reason fields instead of requiring a found member, and inspect residual_unknowns; truncated: false does not establish resolved ownership. #8368.
  • close_binary retains investigation records; use reset_investigation to clear them. Cross-process recovery requires an explicitly acknowledged Evidence-bundle checkpoint. #8361, issue morluto/rea#8347. Thanks @gensokyo-dev.
  • compare_functions adds the matched function_match.method: "same-entry" variant with required entry_address when authenticated artifact, architecture, mapping, slice and entry commitments agree despite a rename. Refresh schemas and keep name/identity changes separate from entry correspondence. #8263, issue morluto/rea#8213. Thanks @gensokyo-dev.
  • inspect_native_load_image NativeAOT metadata_recovery uses a status-discriminated union: complete reports require reason: null; partial/not-applicable reports carry {code, ...} instead of prose reasons. Branch on codes and their fields while preserving historical/optional-extension strings. Invalid budgets now produce typed input/resource errors. #8053.
  • trace_javascript_semantics adds influences edges and moves direct literal-argument seeds from call-site to expression nodes. Relevant unknown dependencies can change found to partial: handle that status and inspect unknowns. Reanalyze retained application graphs to obtain the new edges; old graphs stay readable. #7939, issue morluto/rea#7500. Thanks @Rinkia.
  • inspect_managed_members changes signature_resolution from type-names-v1 to type-names-v2 and appends readable names to signature operands, such as class:4 (Fixture.Program). Branch on the resolution version, accept annotated v2 strings and preserve historical v1/null displays. Signature digests are unchanged; InternalCall methods remain runtime-provided boundaries. #5309, #9547, issue morluto/rea#5149. Thanks @elricfd, @gensokyo-dev, @ln-bodicherla, @Plastic-time.
  • MCP calls default to 1,024 combined array elements/object members; excess input is rejected before dispatch with nonretryable resource_constraint, resource: "transport" and reported_limits.max_input_elements. For larger inline calls, set REA_MCP_MAX_INPUT_ELEMENTS to a digit-only positive decimal safe integer (1–9007199254740991) and restart, or select retained/file inputs. Fractional/exponent spellings are invalid; inputs are never truncated. #7905, issue morluto/rea#7882. Thanks @gensokyo-dev.
  • inspect_macho text names/paths can be null for non-UTF-8 bytes, with name_bytes_hex, segment_bytes_hex and path_bytes_hex alternatives; unsafe numeric sizes/offsets/alignments can be null with exact companion fields, and delayed-init options are numeric. Refresh schemas and preserve these facts; absent byte fields in older Evidence remain unrecorded. #7952, issue morluto/rea#7391. Thanks @coygeek.
  • Environment file-source errors move from the generic evidence issue path to capture for environment-build and source.path for environment bundle build/export. Missing/non-file/invalid UTF-8/JSON sources return invalid_request with invalid_value/invalid_format issues and inspect_input_contract recovery; repair the selected file. #7900, issue morluto/rea#7523. Thanks @coygeek.
  • Selected unpaired UTF-16 surrogates now make TOON, Markdown, CSV and TSV output fail with invalid_request and a nonzero exit even after a successful operation; Markdown also refuses NUL. Use --format json or --format yaml to preserve these values rather than accepting substituted text. #7852, issue morluto/rea#7592, issue morluto/rea#7593. Thanks @coygeek.
  • Reconstruction evidence_bundle inputs reject authenticated standalone records as invalid_request with field-level issues instead of accepting/misclassifying them as damaged bundles. Bundle-file import/export/comparison reports invalid_request/invalid_input with details.reason: "wrong-kind". Supply a canonical bundle from export_evidence_bundle; tampered data remains an integrity failure. #7895, issue morluto/rea#7562. Thanks @coygeek.
  • Environment coverage can use method-replay for non-GET safe reads. Clock-generating write plans without a global clock anchor now return served: false, reason: "unknown-clock" instead of being served. Extend reason handling and recapture usable start times/completed durations before replaying those writes. #7834, 63220edcc3b4, issue morluto/rea#7387. Thanks @coygeek.
  • Ghidra returns not-in-listing for in-memory addresses lacking listed instructions or defined data, replacing the former undecodable interpretation. Accept the new status and recreate snapshots after the raw-profile change; retain old embedded Evidence as history. #7823, issue morluto/rea#7490. Thanks @Entrpi.
  • capture_process_scenario/CLI process capture rejects columns: 1 before target launch; update both initial terminal widths and every resize to at least 2. Sensitive interaction data is empty with sensitive/input_bytes, and raw echoes are redacted. Recapture both sides before comparing older private-input Evidence. #7848, #7804, issue morluto/rea#7582, issue morluto/rea#7782. Thanks @coygeek, @gensokyo-dev.
  • Managed setup registrations bind the selected absolute runtime and REA entry script, including package-runner cache paths. Keep that installation available; unknown registrations need confirmed manual repair. Setup no longer returns prewarm or emits its progress events. c77735fa83a6. Thanks @gensokyo-dev.
  • macOS Hopper launch paths/arguments reject double quotes, backslashes and line breaks before handoff. Select literal-safe prepared paths/loader arguments or a compatible provider. #7732, issue morluto/rea#7727. Thanks @gensokyo-dev.
  • New JavaScript application graphs omit the derived package renderer property. Use renderer_declarations for source fields and package-load edges for the selected candidate; historical Evidence remains readable. 5cf7133f823b.
  • Replace Cutter --json=true/false with --json-output=true/false; bare --json still selects CLI result formatting. Replace rea compare-bundles with rea compare; MCP compare_bundles is unchanged. 567ea4b1e8bb, 3515b262218f.
  • JEB engine refusals report execution_failure rather than unsupported_target; inspect retained upstream diagnostics for recovery. c4cdb7658b0e.
  • ASAR file occurrences may contain unpacked: false. Accept an optional boolean; absent historical fields remain unknown. 60834e391c3f.
  • Non-loopback MCP HTTP binds require MCP_ALLOWED_HOSTS or rea mcp --http --allowed-hosts. Bundle names must be unchanged HTTP hostnames within the 63-octet DNS-label limit. #7172. Thanks @DeryFerd, @gensokyo-dev.
  • binary_session {} is compact by default and omits provider availability diagnostics/remediation, target-support diagnostics/capabilities and tool-group surface/effects/annotations. Request {"detail":"complete"} for these facts and recovery guidance. #7127, issue morluto/rea#7109. Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji.
  • Native input errors now include details.operation and field-level details.issues. Supply a nonempty trace_native_values.procedure; CLI trace-native-values rejects an empty procedure before opening the target as VALIDATION_ERROR with fieldErrors, while MCP reports invalid_request at procedure. #7889, issue morluto/rea#7572. Thanks @coygeek.
  • Readable JavaScript members that normalize to one logical path now stop analyze_javascript_application with artifact_operation_failed, details.reason: "path" and a partial inventory instead of selecting one occurrence. Inspect the inventory and analyze a copy without collisions; all-unavailable colliding sources remain unknown. #7864, issue morluto/rea#7626. Thanks @coygeek.
  • trace_web_module_imports with an empty/relative REA_BROWSER_EXECUTABLE now reports capability_unavailable with invalid_browser_executable instead of missing_browser_executable, retaining details.diagnostics.executable. Set an absolute Chromium path; an unset selection still means missing. #7903, issue morluto/rea#7639. Thanks @coygeek.
  • Unresolvable import-reference-source roots, capture scenario files and web-network capture/Protobuf descriptor paths (ELOOP/ENAMETOOLONG) now report invalid_request/invalid_input with responsible field/host-code issues rather than generic execution/unreadable-output failures. Repair the symlink chain or choose a shorter path. #7847, #7814, issue morluto/rea#7607, issue morluto/rea#7636. Thanks @Abelo9996, @coygeek.
  • close_binary with snapshot_path on a profileless/archive target now returns nonretryable capability_unavailable instead of evidence_integrity_mismatch, writes no snapshot and leaves the session open. Export needed Evidence, then close again without snapshot_path. #7837, issue morluto/rea#7311. Thanks @coygeek.
  • adb local-file refusals occur before device commands: missing/non-file/uninspectable local_path/apk_path and file-blocked output_directory return invalid_request with field issues/host codes; output-producing calls formerly used unreadable_output. Supply regular source files and a directory path whose parents are directories. #7885, issue morluto/rea#7549, issue morluto/rea#7550. Thanks @coygeek.
  • Evidence writes failing with EFBIG, ENOSPC or EDQUOT now report nonretryable resource_constraint, details.reason: "storage-limit", path and optional system_code instead of execution_failure/io. Raise file-size allowance or free space/quota before retrying; a failed staged-file cleanup remains cleanup_incomplete. #7887, issue morluto/rea#7573. Thanks @coygeek.
  • PE signers add optional nullable unsigned_attribute_oids: fresh null means absent, an OID array means present, and omission in old Evidence is unrecorded. Unsigned attributes produce coverage.status: "partial" because nested signatures/timestamp contents remain undecoded; accept the new field and limitations. #7920, issue morluto/rea#7767. Thanks @Rinkia.
  • Valid ASN.1 exceeding certificate nesting/OID allowances now returns nonretryable resource_constraint rather than invalid_request/invalid_input, with max_asn1_depth/max_oid_bytes. These format/resource bounds have no runtime override; retain the diagnostics and use an independently suitable parser when necessary. #9932.
  • trace_call_path/build_call_path require discovery and observed-result fields in search_scope. An exhausted observed graph can still yield goal_status: "unknown" when provider discovery is incomplete. Refresh result schemas and workflow-profile expectations instead of equating graph exhaustion with complete discovery. #8443.
  • inspect_native_instruction adds containing_instruction for not-instruction-boundary and containing_data for data, with address/extent/identity/provenance. Accept these facts; omission in old Evidence is unrecorded. The Ghidra profile changes: reopen and save a fresh snapshot. #8777.
  • trace_native_values adds required page_truncated and tagged traversal coverage. truncated combines page omission with traversal truncation; exactly filling a budget alone no longer proves truncation. Inspect page coverage before fetching another page and traversal reason before raising budgets. #8309, issue morluto/rea#8283. Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji.
  • batch_decompile.addresses has a separate hard ceiling of 20,000 entries. Split larger batches into calls of at most that size; increasing the configurable MCP transport allowance cannot remove this operation limit. #5937, issue morluto/rea#5930. Thanks @dakjdakd.
  • close_binary can retire the target while retaining cleanup owners: cleanup_incomplete may have recovery.kind: "retry_cleanup" despite retryable: false. Keep the same connection open and call close_binary again without snapshot_path to retry owned cleanup. #8329, issue morluto/rea#8211. Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji.
  • Unconfirmed cleanup in inspect_address_context, get_call_graph or trace_call_path now produces tool-level cleanup_incomplete rather than a successful partial result or cancellation. Keep admitted partial Evidence and follow the returned same-session cleanup recovery. #8417, #8516, issue morluto/rea#8369, issue morluto/rea#8416. Thanks @kaoru0822-kitauji.
  • inspect_ntfs_journal omits detailed mftMetadata.record_headers by default and leaves continuation at the first omitted record. Select mft_record_limit from 1 to 32 when those details are needed; inventory counts and explicit coverage remain inline. 1a8a9b749a. Thanks @gensokyo-dev, @N0zoM1z0.
  • Desktop state directories now use application.state_directories[].exists_at_start: null plus existence_error when inspection fails (for example, access denial). Accept the tri-state field and preserve unknown instead of treating it as absence. #9899.

Full contribution record: 1848 commits and 1073 merged PRs.

debpalash/VoiceStudio ↗ 2 releases · 1 stable · 1 prerelease
v0.5.8v0.5.8 — VoiceStudio Stable2026-11-12T08:53:29Z

Download

Platform Download Signing
Windows x64 Installer ⚠️ Unsigned: SmartScreen shows "Windows protected your PC"; choose More info → Run anyway
Windows ARM64 (experimental) Installer ⚠️ Unsigned: SmartScreen shows "Windows protected your PC"; choose More info → Run anyway
macOS Apple Silicon DMG ✅ Signed with Developer ID and notarized by Apple
macOS Intel DMG ✅ Signed with Developer ID and notarized by Apple
Linux x64 AppImage · deb — Linux packages are not code-signed
Android APK · AAB ✅ Signed APK; the AAB is the Play Store bundle
iPhone Unsigned IPA ⚠️ Unsigned: sign it with your own Apple ID to sideload; not a TestFlight build

Highlights

  • OmniVoice GGUF uses AMD, Intel and NVIDIA GPUs through Vulkan on Windows and Linux, and Intel Arc GPUs run OmniVoice
  • Setup falls back to a smaller CPU build when PyTorch downloads fail, and the app falls back to the CPU when the GPU fails
  • Windows loads models one file at a time, and an engine crash names its cause and offers Restart on CPU
  • dots.tts voice cloning runs on Windows through audio.cpp, with no Python setup
  • Convert can keep your delivery and takes video; Dubbing exports subtitles first and moves lines between speakers

OmniVoice now uses your AMD, Intel or NVIDIA GPU, and VoiceStudio gets much harder to break. OmniVoice GGUF runs on any Vulkan GPU on Windows and Linux, sizes its model to your card and can stay loaded between requests (opt-in, Settings → Performance), and Intel Arc GPUs run OmniVoice too. When something goes wrong, the app now keeps going: setup offers a much smaller CPU build when the PyTorch download keeps failing, retries stalled downloads, and VoiceStudio moves to the CPU with a notice when the GPU fails. On Windows, models load one file at a time to avoid a crash on first launch, and an engine crash names the library behind it and offers Restart on CPU.

This release also brings dots.tts voice cloning to Windows with no Python setup, Convert that keeps your delivery and takes video, dubbing that exports subtitles first and moves lines between speakers, a voicestudio terminal command, background updates, a new macOS installer window, and hundreds of fixes to dubbing, setup, audiobooks and the phone apps.

The new macOS installer window (#4001).

Updating from 0.5.7: Take the in-app update offered by 0.5.7, or install over your existing app; voices, projects and settings are kept. On first launch the local runtime updates its packages in place, including the PyTorch 2.10 security update (#3884)

Updating from a 0.5.8 beta: Nothing to do: the Beta channel rolls forward onto 0.5.8 automatically. To stop getting betas, switch to Stable in Settings → Updates → Update channel.

Running from source: The Gradio demo and model training are no longer installed by default; add the ui or train extra if you use them (#4027)

Changed

  • Smaller install: Gradio and WebDataset are no longer installed by default (about 81 MB less); the Gradio demo and model training now use the ui and train extras (#4027)
  • The macOS installer window has a new branded background, larger icons and a clean volume name without the processor suffix (#4001)
  • The backend starts lighter and faster: speech-recognition engines are checked in a short-lived helper process instead of being loaded at startup, while broken installs are still reported as unavailable (#3924, #3946)
  • The app opens with less code to load: the audio and video players load with the first clip played and returning users skip the first-run setup screens, cutting the startup bundle by about 6%, and a new offline benchmark with a CI check on bundle growth tracks startup, memory, CPU and bundle size (#3924, #3940, #3943)
  • Settings → VoiceStudio account lists what to sync and your devices as compact, aligned rows with icons, a Pro badge with one Get Pro link for locked items, and device rows that show OS and when each was last active (#3937)
  • The runtime setup screen fits one window: one status line, the install folder with free space, one main action (or the exact fix, such as choosing another folder when the drive is full), with language, region, Python and remote options under Options and recovery tools under More fixes (#3934)
  • OmniVoice GGUF can keep its runtime loaded between requests to skip the reload before each generation, as an opt-in under Settings → Performance (#3965, #3654)
  • The audio.cpp runtime updates to v0.9.4; installed Breeze-TTS-2, dots.tts and Sortformer models keep working without a re-download, and the previous runtime keeps running them until you install the update (#3129)
  • Updates now download in the background and install when you quit, with a compact title-bar pill for progress, Restart and Retry, clearer error messages and a restart prompt when work is running (#3928)
  • The top bar is more compact: the account button is just your profile photo, or a person icon when signed out, and the GitHub star count is shortened (#3922)
  • Product analytics moved from PostHog to Supermetry, still only after a yes at the first-run prompt, and now also records which engine ran each generation and transcription (#3902)
  • Restyled Settings → VoiceStudio account to match the home page: one account card with your plan and storage, the email shown once, and a storage bar visible at 0% (#3885)
  • Account sign-in is available in every build, still off until you turn it on, and now starts on the VoiceStudio dashboard with a separate Sign up option (#3850)
  • The Pro page in the app now shows the correct $199 Lifetime price (#3831)
  • Mac releases use an Apple API key for notarization instead of an account password (#3775) — thanks @velixio!
  • Source development now prepares the local API environment before launching Electron (#3637)
  • Remove obsolete screenshot and source-archive helpers that targeted the retired UI layout (#3623)
  • Removed unused development tools (pytest-cov, Turborepo and a duplicate TypeScript) from the repository and fixed the remaining broken documentation links (#3571, #2603, #2903) — thanks @themrbeasley and @marciok!
  • Workflows open at /workflows framed to their steps, Calls opens at /calls, and an unknown address shows Page not found (#3547)
  • Projects lists projects under All projects with voices, transcripts, takes and exports grouped under Library; saving a dub now lives in Dubbing (#3547)
  • The OmniVoice sidecar availability check no longer imports the model, so backend startup reconcile finishes about 6 s sooner (#3534) — thanks @adrianchen91!
  • The first-run install screen is cleaner: one progress bar, a step checklist that shows the package being installed, and Cancel and the log inside the progress card (#3534) — thanks @meisolated!

Fixed

  • The invisible watermark no longer adds a faint DC offset to pauses, and the startup warm-up now finds an already-downloaded watermark model (#2818, #4028)
  • Windows now loads model weights one at a time by default, so the first launch no longer hits the threaded-loader crash (#3767, #3772, #4024, #4026)
  • Intel Arc (XPU) GPUs run OmniVoice as GPU-accelerated instead of reporting a CPU fallback, and load it in bfloat16 instead of float16, which sounded choppy on an Arc 140V (OMNIVOICE_XPU_DTYPE overrides it) (#4022) — thanks @mrnitrox!
  • CosyVoice 3 GGUF uses your Vulkan GPU again instead of falling back to CPU; its device check misread the runtime's GPU list (#3980, #4009) — thanks @tera-png!
  • An Ascend NPU whose extension does not load automatically is detected again; checking for a CPU choice no longer hides it (#4007)
  • Restart on CPU after a graphics driver crash no longer loads the GPU driver again, even when a device override was saved in settings (#4006)

CI

  • macOS release signing now retries transient Apple timestamp-service failures instead of failing the build (#4017)
  • The UI crawler in CI no longer waits on requests cancelled by a page reload, stops within seconds when the app stops answering, and spreads its screens evenly across shards (#4008)
  • Release builds can sign the Windows installers through SignPath once it is set up; until then nothing changes (#4004)
  • The iOS CI suite reaches Home's lazily loaded cards, gives each UI test its own time limit instead of re-running the whole suite, and starts its local test servers in seconds (#4000)
  • Later beta release notes now name contributors who are new since the previous beta before the full list (#3999)
  • The iOS UI smoke suite waits for every screen and control on slow runners and retries a failed test once, so it no longer fails at random (#3982)
  • Pull requests wait as drafts with heavy CI skipped until their opener signs the CLA, which now has a 3-day deadline, and a new agent guide helps AI-assisted pull requests arrive merge-ready (#3974)
  • The UI crawler waits for lazily loaded code before leaving a screen, and dev servers no longer send /api-reference pages to the backend, fixing a recurring CI flake (#3952)
  • CI skips heavy jobs whose exact inputs already passed, and merge-queue runs skip the Electron jobs a change cannot affect (#3933)
  • Main-process tests now run on macOS and Windows for every pull request, so platform-specific failures surface before a release (#3927)
  • Releases are refused if they carry another channel's update feed, and publishing checks that a beta never becomes the latest release (#3898)

v0.5.8-beta.5v0.5.8-beta.5 — VoiceStudio Prerelease2026-11-12T00:27:59Z

Installer signing

Platform Signing
macOS (Apple Silicon) ✅ Signed with Developer ID and notarized by Apple
macOS (Intel) ✅ Signed with Developer ID and notarized by Apple
Windows x64 ⚠️ Unsigned: SmartScreen shows "Windows protected your PC"; choose More info → Run anyway
Windows ARM64 ⚠️ Unsigned: SmartScreen shows "Windows protected your PC"; choose More info → Run anyway
Linux x64 — Linux packages are not code-signed

Phone downloads

Android: signed APK · Play bundle.

iPhone: unsigned sideload IPA. Sign with your own Apple ID to install; this is not a TestFlight build.

Windows avoids the crash while loading models, Intel Arc GPUs run OmniVoice, and OmniVoice GGUF resident mode now works in release builds. OmniVoice GGUF runs on AMD, Intel and NVIDIA GPUs through Vulkan on Windows and Linux, dots.tts arrives on Windows with no Python setup, and a failing PyTorch download or a broken GPU no longer leaves you without a working app.

New since beta.4: Windows loads model weights one at a time by default and names the library behind a crash, and Restart on CPU no longer touches the GPU driver; installers bundle tts-server, so OmniVoice GGUF resident mode (Settings → Performance) works; Intel Arc GPUs run OmniVoice in bfloat16; CosyVoice 3 GGUF finds your Vulkan GPU again; the watermark no longer adds a faint offset to silences; Ascend NPUs are detected again; a new macOS installer window; and a default install about 81 MB smaller.

Testing wanted: these get their first real-hardware test in this beta, so reports are welcome: OmniVoice GGUF resident mode with the bundled tts-server, Intel Arc (XPU) GPUs, Restart on CPU after a graphics driver crash, the new Windows default of loading model weights one at a time, and the new macOS DMG window.

Thanks: @mrnitrox (Intel Arc support, #4022) and @khawajaJunaid (Intel Mac Radeon docs, #4014) for code; @tera-png (#3980), @Imammahmuda1804 (#3767), @oryx-system (#3772), @pimeNtex (#4024) and @Patrix9999 (#2818) for bug reports.

Download

Windows x64 Installer
Windows ARM64 (experimental) Installer
macOS Apple Silicon DMG
macOS Intel DMG
Linux x64 AppImage · deb
Android APK · AAB
iPhone Unsigned IPA (sideload with your own Apple ID)

Upgrading: Install over your existing app; voices, projects and settings are kept.

Highlights

  • OmniVoice GGUF uses AMD, Intel and NVIDIA GPUs through Vulkan on Windows and Linux
  • dots.tts voice cloning now runs on Windows through audio.cpp, with no Python setup
  • Convert can keep your delivery, changing only the voice
  • Dubbing exports subtitles before dubbing and moves lines between speakers
  • Setup falls back to the CPU build when PyTorch downloads fail, and the app falls back to the CPU when the GPU fails

Changed

  • Smaller install: Gradio and WebDataset are no longer installed by default (about 81 MB less); the Gradio demo and model training now use the ui and train extras (#4027)
  • The macOS installer window has a new branded background, larger icons and a clean volume name without the processor suffix (#4001)
  • The backend starts lighter and faster: speech-recognition engines are checked in a short-lived helper process instead of being loaded at startup, while broken installs are still reported as unavailable (#3924, #3946)
  • The app opens with less code to load: the audio and video players load with the first clip played and returning users skip the first-run setup screens, cutting the startup bundle by about 6%, and a new offline benchmark with a CI check on bundle growth tracks startup, memory, CPU and bundle size (#3924, #3940, #3943)
  • Settings → VoiceStudio account lists what to sync and your devices as compact, aligned rows with icons, a Pro badge with one Get Pro link for locked items, and device rows that show OS and when each was last active (#3937)
  • The runtime setup screen fits one window: one status line, the install folder with free space, one main action (or the exact fix, such as choosing another folder when the drive is full), with language, region, Python and remote options under Options and recovery tools under More fixes (#3934)
  • OmniVoice GGUF can keep its runtime loaded between requests to skip the reload before each generation, as an opt-in under Settings → Performance (#3965, #3654)
  • The audio.cpp runtime updates to v0.9.4; installed Breeze-TTS-2, dots.tts and Sortformer models keep working without a re-download, and the previous runtime keeps running them until you install the update (#3129)
  • Updates now download in the background and install when you quit, with a compact title-bar pill for progress, Restart and Retry, clearer error messages and a restart prompt when work is running (#3928)
  • The top bar is more compact: the account button is just your profile photo, or a person icon when signed out, and the GitHub star count is shortened (#3922).
  • Product analytics moved from PostHog to Supermetry, still only after a yes at the first-run prompt, and now also records which engine ran each generation and transcription. (#3902)
  • Restyled Settings → VoiceStudio account to match the home page: one account card with your plan and storage, the email shown once, and a storage bar visible at 0%. (#3885)
  • Account sign-in is available in every build, still off until you turn it on, and now starts on the VoiceStudio dashboard with a separate Sign up option (#3850)
  • The Pro page in the app now shows the correct $199 Lifetime price (#3831)
  • Mac releases use an Apple API key for notarization instead of an account password. (#3775) — thanks @velixio!
  • Source development now prepares the local API environment before launching Electron (#3637)
  • Remove obsolete screenshot and source-archive helpers that targeted the retired UI layout. (#3623)
  • Removed unused development tools (pytest-cov, Turborepo and a duplicate TypeScript) from the repository and fixed the remaining broken documentation links (#3571, #2603, #2903) — thanks @themrbeasley and @marciok!
  • Workflows open at /workflows framed to their steps, Calls opens at /calls, and an unknown address shows Page not found (#3547)
  • Projects lists projects under All projects with voices, transcripts, takes and exports grouped under Library; saving a dub now lives in Dubbing (#3547)
  • The OmniVoice sidecar availability check no longer imports the model, so backend startup reconcile finishes about 6 s sooner (#3534) — thanks @adrianchen91!
  • The first-run install screen is cleaner: one progress bar, a step checklist that shows the package being installed, and Cancel and the log inside the progress card (#3534) — thanks @meisolated!

Added

  • Installers now bundle omnivoice.cpp's tts-server, so the opt-in OmniVoice GGUF resident mode (Settings → Performance) works in release builds and keeps falling back to the per-request runtime when unavailable (#4005, #3654)
  • Qwen Base can deliver audio during generation on the speech WebSocket (#3992) — thanks @kellerkind84!
  • OpenAI-compatible PCM speech can stream during synthesis, with native Qwen3-TTS Base support on Apple Silicon and provisioned NVIDIA systems (#3976) — thanks @kellerkind84!
  • The account menu in the window corner shows your profile photo, email, plan and cloud storage, with a Manage plan link (#3955)
  • The VoiceStudio account card has a larger avatar that opens your profile page to change the photo, an inline display-name editor, a plan badge with icon, an iconized storage meter, and icon buttons for refresh and sign out (#3948)
  • Pronunciation dictionaries can now belong to a single voice: switch the pronunciation settings to This voice and its entries win over the language dictionary whenever that voice speaks (#3873)
  • New voicestudio terminal command: use voices, speech, transcription, dubbing and models from your terminal, with VoiceStudio starting in the background when needed, plus Tab completion (#3828) — thanks @mostafa-ismail-2004!
  • An OpenAI-compatible LLM provider with an API key can now use plain HTTP on a trusted LAN IP address, through a new per-provider opt-in that is off by default (#3811)
  • Ming-3.0-tts, an opt-in 0.5B voice-cloning engine for Chinese, English, Japanese, Korean, French, German and Spanish on NVIDIA GPUs, installs in its own environment from Model Catalogue (#3793)
  • Convert voice takes a video: its voice is swapped with Keep my delivery and you get the video back, keeping picture and background when they can be preserved, otherwise the converted audio with a message (#3750)
  • Dubbing can now keep the original transcript and speak it in the source language, skipping translation, from the segment menu (#3747)
  • Experimental native Windows ROCm setup for RDNA 1 gfx1010 GPUs (RX 5700 / 5700 XT) runs PyTorch TTS on the GPU, with speech recognition on the CPU (#3734) — thanks @sdf123098!
  • Dub video exports can use your graphics card's encoder (NVENC, Quick Sync, AMF, VideoToolbox) as an opt-in, falling back to software if it fails, and a Maximum resolution setting scales large videos down on export (#3732)
  • Native Windows ROCm setup supports RDNA 2 RX 6000 GPUs with verified Torch speech, isolated ASR and safer package repair, and installs a hash-pinned HIP CTranslate2 build so faster-whisper transcription runs on the GPU (#3712, #3947) — thanks @dutch2005!
  • Tools > Sound effects generates sound effects from a text description with the opt-in MOSS-SoundEffect v2.0 engine (NVIDIA GPU, Apache-2.0, desktop and web only; installs only when you click Install) (#3577)
  • The Nix flake can now build and run the Linux app with nix run ., keeps the NixOS FHS shell, and adds an Apple Silicon dev shell (#2699) — thanks @eaves-dropper!
  • Clone and Design now render at each engine's best quality by default, and offer faster settings with a time estimate only when a take is measured to be slow on your computer (#3929)
  • Add GUI selection and bounded discovery of existing Python environments with explicit confirmation and consent-gated dependency repair. (#3701) — thanks @EriconYu!
  • While signed in with account features on, the app sends a periodic check-in with its name, version and operating system; it stops on sign-out or when account features are off. (#3892)
  • The first-run setup screen shows the detected GPU and whether the install uses Metal, CUDA, ROCm or the CPU build. (#3891)
  • The first-run setup screen shows the app version and channel, offers a pending update before the runtime download, and keeps install help visible. (#3890)
  • An available desktop update can be downloaded and installed from a notice on any screen, including first-run setup, and update errors read plainly instead of showing raw server responses (#3881)
  • Development cleanup removes project dependencies, environments, build outputs and default app data, with a dry-run preview (#3877)
  • Dub, batch dub and Tools → Translate offer Kabyle (Taqbaylit) as a translation target (#3740) — thanks @Aksel103!
  • Add per-engine local model folders with authorized discovery, compatibility checks and non-destructive default reset. (#3702) — thanks @EriconYu!
  • Clone takes can be saved as MP3 as well as WAV from the save button's format menu (#3694) — thanks @lucassmonn!
  • Audiobook and Stories scripts take a per-line [emotion:...] direction (one line scared, the next excited) on IndexTTS2, CosyVoice 3 and VoxCPM2; other engines and the phone apps skip the tag instead of reading it aloud (#3664) — thanks @sidmax7!
  • Settings → Account has a Refresh button that reloads your profile and plan (#3850)
  • When the large PyTorch download keeps failing, setup now offers a much smaller CPU version so you still get a working app (#3846)
  • VoiceStudio now tests your GPU on startup and uses the CPU, with a notice, if the GPU fails (#3845)
  • Crash reports now show what was running and how much memory was free, so out-of-memory crashes are easy to spot (#3844)
  • Install and startup failures now name a cause, show the fix first and fill in their own bug report; setup warns about Smart App Control and tests Python before the large download (#3810)
  • Add an Arch Linux development source package with package-managed updates (#3771)
  • Add Android release updates through Obtainium and a publisher-operated F-Droid repository. (#3765)
  • Prepare a bundled, signed runtime and persistent folder grants for private Mac App Store validation. (#3755)
  • Allow explicit external TestFlight beta uploads while keeping internal testing as the default. (#3749)
  • Let Android and iOS users try models below the recommended RAM, with clear warnings and the existing licence and download checks. (#3738)
  • Add native Donate, Pro information and Other Apps pages to iOS and Android, with offline artwork and translated copy. (#3728)
  • Opt-in Settings switch fills [pause] markers and audiobook gaps with the take's own room tone instead of digital silence (#3615, #3611) — thanks @carmoe-prog for the idea!
  • OmniVoice GGUF uses AMD, Intel and NVIDIA GPUs through Vulkan on Windows and Linux, sizes its model to the GPU, adds a model quant picker with Download, and offers a one-time Use GPU prompt when PyTorch has no GPU (#3615, #3599) — thanks @dutch2005!
  • Galician (Galego) is now available as an app language on desktop and mobile (#3594) — thanks @B1NAR10!
  • Supermetry joins as VoiceStudio's first Strategic Sponsor, shown in SPONSORS.md, the README and the in-app Support page (#3583)
  • dots.tts now runs on Windows too: the new audio.cpp dots.tts engine clones voices in many languages with no Python setup, and Model Catalogue installs and removes each audio.cpp model on its own (#3574, #2665) — thanks @HuntingSuccubus!
  • Convert can now keep your delivery: turn on Keep my delivery to change only the voice while your timing, pauses and intonation stay as recorded (needs CosyVoice 3) (#3573, #3421)
  • A transcribed video can be exported before dubbing: subtitles of the edited transcript, or the original video with burned-in subtitles (#3555, #3393) — thanks @EntityinArray!
  • Dubbing lines can be moved to another speaker or a new speaker, one at a time or in a selection, and take that speaker's voice (#3555, #3515) — thanks @iamhrhp!
  • Audiobook scripts show a live chapter outline: click a # heading to jump there, see spoken-word counts, and insert a new chapter from the markup toolbar (#3541) — thanks @mvanhorn!
  • Urdu speech reads numbers, years, percentages and decimals in Urdu words instead of raw digits (#3540) — thanks @maatitechnologies-hue!
  • Transcribe adds an Identify speakers switch in accurate mode, with speaker labels in segments and exports (#3535) — thanks @adrianchen91!
  • Dubbing can jump between flagged transcript segments, including offscreen rows, with Previous and Next issue controls (#3535) — thanks @Thakay!
  • Voice Cloning's Emotion button is always on the composer and adapts to the engine — sliders on IndexTTS2, a written direction on CosyVoice 3 and VoxCPM2, and a clear explanation elsewhere; Insert menus gray out tags the engine would read aloud (#3422)
  • Nix / NixOS users can build and run VoiceStudio from source with the new community-maintained flake (#2961) — thanks @shamil2!
  • Opper is available as a named LLM provider for translation and rewriting, used only after you select it and add a key (#2668) — thanks @Felixkw12!
  • API Route is now available as a named LLM provider for translation and rewriting, used only after you select it and add a key (#2596) — thanks @DennyHo0917!

Fixed

  • The invisible watermark no longer adds a faint DC offset to pauses, and the startup warm-up now finds an already-downloaded watermark model (#2818, #4028)
  • Windows now loads model weights one at a time by default, so the first launch no longer hits the threaded-loader crash (#3767, #3772, #4024, #4026)
  • Intel Arc (XPU) GPUs run OmniVoice as GPU-accelerated instead of reporting a CPU fallback, and load it in bfloat16 instead of float16, which sounded choppy on an Arc 140V (OMNIVOICE_XPU_DTYPE overrides it) (#4022) — thanks @mrnitrox!
  • CosyVoice 3 GGUF uses your Vulkan GPU again instead of falling back to CPU; its device check misread the runtime's GPU list (#3980, #4009) — thanks @tera-png!
  • An Ascend NPU whose extension does not load automatically is detected again; checking for a CPU choice no longer hides it (#4007)
  • Restart on CPU after a graphics driver crash no longer loads the GPU driver again, even when a device override was saved in settings (#4006)
  • Windows engine crashes now name the fault and the library that crashed, offer Restart on CPU after a graphics driver crash, and load model weights one at a time after a crash in the weight loader (#4002)
  • Update MLX to 0.32.4 to address a shutdown crash after Apple Silicon speech generation (#3991) — thanks @kellerkind84!
  • Chromium and Electron switches given to the Linux AppImage or .deb reach the app again, and the bundled voicestudio command no longer stops macOS and Linux installers from being built (#3996)
  • The dubbing error notice links directly to the transcript segment with missing or unreadable speech (#3988) — thanks @isnaenihidayat!
  • Large dubbing stem and segment ZIP downloads no longer hold the entire archive in backend memory (#3986) — thanks @isnaenihidayat!
  • Audiobook chapters and dub segments wait for a speech engine that is briefly busy streaming instead of failing; if it stays busy, the audiobook stops with a clear error and keeps its progress for a retry (#3987)
  • Apple Silicon speech requests now retry cleanly when a native Qwen stream is using the MLX model (#3981) — thanks @kellerkind84!
  • CosyVoice 3 GGUF now explains why it is unavailable (runtime, model files or device probe) and falls back to the runtime's CPU path when the Vulkan probe fails (#3980)
  • Older NVIDIA cards (GTX 900/1000 series) now get an accurate explanation and a pointer to the Vulkan engines instead of advice to install a cu128 PyTorch build (#3978)
  • Storage, setup download and wheel prefetch sizes move to the next unit instead of showing "1024 KB" or "1024 MB" (#3972) — thanks @kevin9327!
  • Dubbing now tells you which speakers had too little clean speech to clone and use the default voice, and shows transcription warnings such as a missing diarization model (#3419, #3971)
  • Dubbing now retries a failed background separation on a lighter setting and then on the CPU, and tells you right away if the original background could not be kept (#3751, #3970)
  • Voice cloning decodes non-WAV references for add-on engines and keeps cached source paths relative (#3709, #3958) — thanks @samay-hash!
  • Setup now retries a download that hits a temporary server error (such as a 504) and restarts one that gets no data for two minutes, instead of failing or waiting fifteen (#3882, #3950, #3956, #3957) — thanks @velixio!
  • Android: a phone voice clone stopped by leaving the app or rotating the phone now says so and keeps the sample and name for Retry (#3953) — thanks @hkarmoush!
  • The Nix flake build no longer fails on unrelated changes: its dependency install is built from the package manifests alone and is deterministic (#3949)
  • The desktop helper no longer wakes every 8 ms while a dictation shortcut is set, so the installed app stays idle in the background (#3924, #3945)
  • Updates no longer leave a second copy of the installed update (about 270 MB) in the updater cache; a downloaded newer update is kept (#3939)
  • Sidebar tabs such as Design and Saved no longer cut off to a single letter next to the macOS window buttons (#3926)
  • MCP tools now say why VoiceStudio refused a request, such as a model licence to accept, instead of a bare HTTP status, with secrets and home paths removed (#3916) — thanks @meosbrand!
  • The Windows uninstaller now offers (default No) to also delete the downloaded runtime and models, keeping your voices and projects. (#3911)
  • Generate Dub and Batch no longer stop with "Choose a supported language" for Chinese (Simplified) or Chinese (Traditional) on engines that speak Chinese (#3685, #3883) — thanks @victorberinde2-debug!
  • The Snap now starts under XWayland by default instead of crashing on native Wayland (set VOICESTUDIO_SNAP_OZONE_PLATFORM=wayland to opt back in), and its dictation shortcut helper loads again (#3878, #3879)
  • MOSS-TTS-Nano loads and speaks again with transformers 5 (Docker and app installs) instead of failing to load or producing NaN audio (#3851) — thanks @kanakhinyegor-glitch!
  • The backend no longer crashes with an illegal instruction on CPUs without AVX2: pedalboard is probed in a separate process first and effects are skipped if it cannot load (#3848)
  • Dub now recognizes source languages reported under another spelling, such as Kirghiz, Panjabi, Moroccan Arabic or the old Yiddish code, instead of asking for the language (#3821, #3825) — thanks @Ayushkumarxy!
  • Standard Arabic and regional Arabic names now work on engines that list a single Arabic, such as VoxCPM2, instead of failing with "doesn't support language" (#3816, #3817) — thanks @Vivek1898!
  • Update checks on a custom or unpackaged build without app-update.yml now say the build cannot self-update instead of failing with a raw ENOENT error (#3814)
  • On macOS, bun run dev works with GNU coreutils on PATH, no longer waits on a Keychain prompt at startup, and survives main-process restarts (#3813) — thanks @larrytoube!
  • Apple Silicon: an installed mlx-audio model such as Kokoro loads from disk instead of checking Hugging Face, so the first generation no longer hangs and blocks every later request; a missing model now fails at once with the install step (#3776)
  • Long scripts and texts with [pause] markers keep one designed voice from start to finish instead of switching speakers between chunks (#3730, #3731) — thanks @PyTuH11952!
  • Source checkouts in a non-ASCII folder on Windows (for example a Thai cp874 system) now run bun run dev and bun run setup:api instead of failing to start Python (#1783, #3726) — thanks @Taksin014!
  • Resuming a long audiobook now shows every finished chapter instead of stopping the count at 500 (#3562) — thanks @HelloWorld3326!
  • Inline pronunciation overrides such as [[route|route]] now win over the pronunciation dictionary and are honored in audiobook chapters, which re-render once where they were read out wrongly (#3561) — thanks @HelloWorld3326!
  • Reset and Remove all data never delete a shared model cache, or your data, when the two folders are the same place or one sits inside the other (#3512) — thanks @Pates2004!
  • Clear stale synthesis and model-selection warnings after all required model licences are accepted (#3910)
  • A backend that exits without a traceback now explains itself: the report carries its last 20 stderr lines, hard exits name their caller, and idle model release can no longer end the process (#3904)
  • Desktop updates keep working when GitHub release downloads fail, by retrying through the GitHub API; a failed update check retries within an hour instead of six, and a stalled download is cancelled and retried (#3899)
  • Updated PyTorch to 2.10 to close a remote-code-execution flaw in checkpoint loading (CVE-2026-24747); AMD Linux GPU installs now use ROCm 7.1. (#3884)
