CI

  • macOS release signing now retries transient Apple timestamp-service failures instead of failing the build (#4017)
  • The UI crawler in CI no longer waits on requests cancelled by a page reload, stops within seconds when the app stops answering, and spreads its screens evenly across shards (#4008)
  • Release builds can sign the Windows installers through SignPath once it is set up; until then nothing changes (#4004)
  • The iOS CI suite reaches Home's lazily loaded cards, gives each UI test its own time limit instead of re-running the whole suite, and starts its local test servers in seconds (#4000)
  • Later beta release notes now name contributors who are new since the previous beta before the full list (#3999)
  • The iOS UI smoke suite waits for every screen and control on slow runners and retries a failed test once, so it no longer fails at random (#3982)
  • Pull requests wait as drafts with heavy CI skipped until their opener signs the CLA, which now has a 3-day deadline, and a new agent guide helps AI-assisted pull requests arrive merge-ready (#3974)
  • The UI crawler waits for lazily loaded code before leaving a screen, and dev servers no longer send /api-reference pages to the backend, fixing a recurring CI flake (#3952)
  • CI skips heavy jobs whose exact inputs already passed, and merge-queue runs skip the Electron jobs a change cannot affect (#3933)
  • Main-process tests now run on macOS and Windows for every pull request, so platform-specific failures surface before a release (#3927)
  • Releases are refused if they carry another channel's update feed, and publishing checks that a beta never becomes the latest release. (#3898)
  • CI actions updated: setup-node 7, setup-java 6, deploy-pages 5, CodeQL 4.39.3 everywhere, Vulkan SDK and Docker login actions. (#3893, #3894, #3895, #3896, #3897)
  • Release builds retry macOS packaging when Apple's notarization service drops a connection, instead of failing the release. (#3889)

studio-v2.61.0Reasonix Studio v2.61.0 Stable2026-11-12T08:28:13Z

本版是最后一个版本：移除软件内的反馈建议、崩溃报告上传和匿名统计，并修复文件树、提交历史等一批问题。

新增

  • 工作台的文件树可以拖动边缘调整宽度，宽度会被记住，双击边缘恢复默认 (376376843)
  • 新增「进程」按钮，点开列出本会话正在运行的后台任务和子智能体 (3807e411d)
  • 新增设置「允许模型直接打开工作区内的文件夹」，默认关闭；打开后模型把本工作区内的文件夹加入项目时不再弹批准卡 #15090

修复

  • 公式不再被裁掉上标的顶部 #15092
  • 没有伪控制台的 Windows 上，终端会说明原因，而不是报"shell 启动失败" #15091
  • 在 Cygwin 或 MSYS 终端里运行时，会提示界面无法在这里运行以及该怎么办 #15091
  • Windows 上安装前端依赖（pnpm install）不再失败 #15067
  • 操作被拒绝时的原因不再被输入框盖住 (f4b815ecd)
  • 读取内核失败时，窗口会说明内核无法连接以及是哪一次读取，而不是只显示"Failed to fetch" #15070
  • 窄栏里「更改」标题行的按钮换行显示，不再挤成竖排并出现横向滚动条 (a717dcd34)
  • 提交历史会在新提交出现、分支变化时自动刷新 (803583e4a)
  • 选了资源管理器后，右边不再留着已经打开的启动菜单 (0a17f7774)
  • 归档长会话时不再逐个规范化其他会话的路径，归档明显更快 (5cd38fde8)
  • Homebrew 安装不再因为 cask 无法读取而失败 #15071
  • 构建改用 Go 1.26.11，修复标准库 net/http 的已知漏洞 GO-2026-6859 (75f560400)

移除

  • 移除软件内的反馈与建议入口、崩溃报告上传和匿名统计；项目不再维护 (21ffa334b)

升级须知

  • 本版之后项目不再维护。软件不会再向维护者发送任何报告或统计，reasonix report send 会被拒绝；更新检查和扩展市场仍会访问原有服务，服务停止后将不可用 (21ffa334b)

<!-- lang:en -->

This is the last release: it removes the in-app feedback, crash report upload and anonymous statistics, and fixes a batch of issues in the file tree, commit history and more.

New

  • The workbench file tree can be resized by dragging its edge; the width is remembered and a double click restores the default (376376843)
  • A new Processes button opens a list of this session's running background jobs and sub-agents (3807e411d)
  • A new setting, "Let the model open folders inside the workspace directly", is off by default; when on, the model adds folders inside this workspace to the project without an approval card #15090

Fixed

  • A display formula no longer loses the top of a raised exponent #15092
  • On a Windows without a pseudoconsole, the terminal says so instead of reporting that the shell failed to start #15091
  • Run from a Cygwin or MSYS terminal, it says the UI cannot run there and what to do instead #15091
  • Installing the frontend dependencies (pnpm install) on Windows no longer fails #15067
  • The reason an action was refused is no longer painted over by the composer (f4b815ecd)
  • When the kernel cannot be reached, the window says so and names the read, instead of showing only "Failed to fetch" #15070
  • In a narrow explorer the Changes header wraps its buttons instead of squeezing into a vertical stack with a horizontal scrollbar (a717dcd34)
  • The commit history refreshes when a commit appears or the branch changes (803583e4a)
  • Choosing the explorer no longer leaves the start menu beside the tree it just opened (0a17f7774)
  • Archiving a long session no longer canonicalizes every other session's path, so it is noticeably faster (5cd38fde8)
  • Homebrew installs no longer fail because the cask cannot be read #15071
  • Builds now use Go 1.26.11, which fixes the known net/http vulnerability GO-2026-6859 (75f560400)

Removed

  • The in-app feedback and suggestion entry, crash report upload and anonymous statistics are removed; the project is no longer maintained (21ffa334b)

Upgrade notes

  • The project is no longer maintained after this release. The app sends no report or statistic to the maintainers, and reasonix report send is refused; update checks and the extension market still use the existing services and will stop working once those are shut down (21ffa334b)

Full Changelog: https://github.com/esengine/DeepSeek-Reasonix/compare/studio-v2.60.0...studio-v2.61.0

macOS 已用 Developer ID 签名并公证，打开时不需要清除隔离属性。Windows 的窗口、内核与安装器都经 Certum 代码签名证书做了 Authenticode 签名。Linux Debian 系从 .deb 安装，Fedora、RHEL、openSUSE 等从 .rpm 安装（sudo dnf install ./ReasonixStudio-linux-amd64.rpm）。x86_64 用文件名带 amd64 的包，aarch64（统信 UOS、麒麟等）用带 arm64 的包。也提供 .AppImage（只提供下载，不会自更新；需要 FUSE 2，或用 --appimage-extract-and-run 启动）。

三个平台都能自更新：Linux 的 .deb 走自己的包（.rpm 的自更新尚未提供，请手动下载新包），Windows 运行下一个安装器，macOS 替换自身 bundle。

thedotmack/claude-mem ↗ 3 releases · 3 stable
v15.18.1 Stable2026-11-12T07:52:21Z

Critical fixes

  • The worker no longer fails to start when the database is busy at startup (#6041). If the My Work refresh record was left as "running" by a restart and the database was locked at that moment, the repair write threw and took down the worker's whole startup, so memory stayed off until the next restart. The repair failure is now logged, and the next scheduled check tries again (whether or not scheduled refresh is on).
  • npx claude-mem update and the daily auto-update keep your setup (#6045). Before this, an unattended update reset a server or server-beta runtime back to the local worker, so capture moved off your server. A legacy install with no saved provider could also have its cloud sync turned off and its Anthropic env keys cleared. Updates now reuse your saved runtime, provider, model and agents. They don't reopen the browser setup or ask the setup questions again, and only ask for a Gemini or OpenRouter key if one is missing. npx claude-mem install is unchanged.
  • My Work summaries stop retrying unusable model answers forever (#6039, thanks @mohitdixit02, reported by @adminwat in #6037). When the model's answer failed validation, wasn't JSON or came back empty, the same unchanged request was resent every minute with no end. One report measured 104,869 calls, and the constant load starved a local Ollama runner. Each request version now gets at most 3 tries with a growing wait, then waits until its input or settings change. The card shows the actual reason. Thanks @balabommablock-cpu for testing and finding the empty-answer case.

Fixes

  • Lower observer cost by default (#6031, thanks @kartik70, closes #5023). The observer's conversation budget drops from 400,000 to 100,000 characters (about 100K to about 26K tokens per turn in the issue's measurement). Installs that still hold the old shipped default move once; a value you set yourself is kept.
  • Redaction: Heroku API keys and Atlassian API tokens are now removed before storage (#6046, thanks @kevin9327).
  • TeamBrain: relayed text can no longer pass as claude-mem's own headings, fences or rules (~~~, ***, ___, setext ===) (#6048, thanks @kevin9327).
  • Work state: GET /api/work-state/export returns the complete history of a list when it is too long for an MCP reply, and shortened MCP reads now say so and point to it (#6049, thanks @Jeremy891102, closes #6043).
  • My Work (thanks @Afloat16):
    • A checked request is unchecked again when a new deliverable revision or published PR changes its result (#5891).
    • The first goals map of a long prompt history runs in checkpointed batches instead of one oversized question that fails and repeats (#6001).
    • Resolving original requests no longer rescans the whole prompt window per conversation (#6022).
  • Smart file read: function signatures end at the real body boundary in every supported language, so destructured parameters and default objects stay in the outline and are searchable (#6029, thanks @Afloat16).
  • Gemini CLI: npx claude-mem gemini install finds a worker installed only in the plugin cache instead of reporting it missing (#6032, thanks @Kirat0201).
  • Memory: replaying an older native memory note uses an index instead of scanning project history inside the write transaction (#6016, thanks @Afloat16).

New examples and docs

  • Obsidian: an opt-in, one-way export of selected memories into an existing vault, never overwriting your notes (#6051, thanks @Jeremy891102, closes #5943).
  • Pipecat: an explicit project-memory pipeline example (#6050, thanks @Jeremy891102, closes #5946).
  • elizaOS: a verified explicit-memory example (#6035, thanks @Kirat0201, closes #5914).
  • ContextForge: a private recall workflow (#6033, thanks @Kirat0201, closes #5935).

View release →

v15.18.0 Stable2026-11-12T03:36:31Z

eve agents get the same memory as Claude Code and Codex

claude-mem/eve now gives an eve agent the full claude-mem memory loop through your cmem.ai account (#6042).

  • Session context: each turn starts with the recent timeline from your memory. It is refreshed every turn under one message id, so it replaces itself instead of piling up.
  • Recall each turn: one search before the turn, as before.
  • Recording is now on by default. After each completed turn the provider sends the prompt, one observation per tool call, and a turn summary to cmem.ai as platform eve. Recorded tool calls and summaries come out of your cmem.ai observer allowance, the same allowance your other clients use. Pass capture: false to keep the old read-only behaviour; session context, recall, search and remember keep working.
    • <private>…</private> regions and secret-shaped text are removed on your deployment before anything is sent, with the same rules the Cowork plugin uses.
    • Replays of the same turn are dropped by cmem.ai (turn-scoped tool call ids and an idempotency key).
    • Each request is sent once. No retry, spool or queue, so an outage never creates spend later. A failure is logged with how many events were accepted.
  • Tools: search, timeline, get_observations (the three-step Smart search) and remember.
  • eve versions: tested on eve 0.84.0 and 1.0.0-rc.9.
  • Template: works on a free-credit Vercel AI Gateway (Claude, falling back to openai/gpt-5-mini when Claude is refused on free credits), or set ANTHROPIC_API_KEY to call Claude directly and EVE_MODEL to pick an exact model. Scheduled runs get the owner's memory, owner email must match exactly, and a morning-digest example schedule ships turned off.

Docs: https://docs.claude-mem.ai/eve-integration

Install choices move to the browser

A standard install now sets up the runtime in the terminal, then opens the local web setup to choose agents and integrations, bundled skills, model, native-memory preferences, optional email updates and telemetry (#5967). Agent shells can pass --setup browser when a person is there, or --setup defer to leave the choices for the first My Work visit. Explicit --provider / --ide installs and npx claude-mem update behave as before.

Also new

  • Gemini CLI: native lifecycle capture with npx claude-mem gemini install|status|uninstall (#5991, thanks @Kirat0201, closes #5949). Docs: https://docs.claude-mem.ai/gemini-cli/setup
  • Knowledge corpus on your API provider (opt-in): CLAUDE_MEM_CORPUS_AGENT=api answers query_corpus through the selected OpenRouter or OpenAI-compatible provider instead of the Claude Agent SDK. The default is unchanged (#5682, thanks @bulanovk).
  • Agent SDK: a project-scoped memory example (#5995, thanks @Kirat0201).

Privacy clarification

  • Prompt-history uploads (#5176): browser setup asks before sending selected prompt history to cmem.ai (fixed in v15.8.3, #5220). Skip this step sends no prompt history. The owner-goals skill can use your existing coding agent to analyze local history and save through the local worker without a separate cmem.ai intent upload. Inference still uses that agent's model provider. Updating does not remove data previously sent.

Fixes

Thanks @Afloat16 for every fix below unless another name is given.

Search and memory

  • Unicode words in long questions still match indexed memories (#5997); canonically equivalent titles match (#5975); literal backslashes in POSIX file history are kept (#5985, thanks @Kirat0201); the observation-only semantic endpoint paginates (#5892).
  • Recall survives repeated project merges (#5990), and Chroma keeps live metadata during a merge and bootstraps large projects without spreading row ids (#5881, #5862).
  • Distinct structured observation content is no longer merged (#5979); colliding manual session identities are kept apart (#5868).
  • Native memory notes: quoted metadata is decoded (#5983), BOM-prefixed Markdown parses (#5976), and frontmatter needs a real top-level closing line (#5845).
  • Knowledge answers in several parts are kept whole (#6002).

Context and server

  • Server context rows obey mode filters (#5993, thanks @0xABCD01) and keep their platforms when prior replies are selected (#6003); cached labels refresh after discovery usage settles (#6006).
  • The server keeps platform scope for unlinked manual memories (#5875), native receipt identity through event validation (#5872), event platforms in viewer feeds (#5846), and capture mutation receipts within the key's scope (#5883).
  • Delegated recall keys need read scope (#5856); in-flight generation results are purged before returning (#5858); salvaged summary bodies show in the viewer (#5869).
  • Shutdown stops accepting HTTP before draining connections (#5853).
  • The observer keeps delivery identity when capture is disabled (#5867); sync keeps the outbox when a stopped push is rejected (#5850).

Import and setup

  • Imports keep compressed Codex history (#6000), Gemini text-part provenance and JSONL history edits (#5999, #5865), Unicode in prompt provenance (#5989), distinct legacy Claude prompts (#5982) and legacy observation text (#5854); copied OpenCode fork activity is excluded (#6004).
  • A history import is bounded to its starting rows (#5877); local scans include archived Codex history (#5888); token coverage leaves out observer history and Codex context estimates (#5847, #5886).
  • Exports include the full saved history (#5879); linked data-directory ancestors work for media (#5874); Claude Code rule imports escape spaces (#5852).
  • Telemetry discloses default sharing in the first session (#5884).

My Work

  • Shared-work sync passes are bounded so a hung thread cannot stall owner actions (#6014, thanks @LuckVd), and reader requests have a deadline (#5860, thanks @LuckVd).
  • Session provenance is republished after a failed activity publish (#6010, thanks @bulanovk); failed shared-activity publication is retried (#5870).
  • Owner goals drop unverifiable quotes from a fresh save instead of refusing the whole save (#5992, thanks @0xABCD01); rules-only goal snapshots are reused (#5987); newer focus decisions survive an undo (#5849).
  • Products refresh when local files change (#5887), GitHub products link from SSH push output (#5998), valued git push options parse before targets (#5885), and single-quoted commit subjects are kept literally (#5896).
  • Deliverables keep declarations for previously captured copies (#5973).

Integrations

  • OpenCode: the current V2 built-in tool contract is normalized and failed tool status is kept (#5857, #5855).
  • OMP: native tool semantics and tool-call receipt identity are kept, and hooks install into the selected profile (#5871, #5864, #5876).
  • OpenClaw: JSON5 configuration is accepted, and the selected host config and state paths are used (#5861, #5844).
  • Windsurf: user hook commands survive installation and the MCP server identity is kept for recursion filtering (#5882, #5880).
  • Cursor: MCP argument and result JSON envelopes are decoded (#5848). DSH: the managed transcript path refreshes on reinstall (#5873). T3 Code: TOML plugin state is read before removal (#5851).
  • Smart file reading includes C and C++ header declarations, compact Java record constructors and TypeScript declare functions (#6015, #6012, #6005); XML literals keep their closing tags (#5977).

Redaction and performance

  • Pulumi access tokens and Doppler personal tokens are redacted (#5984, thanks @kevin9327).
  • Telemetry aggregates event statistics on arrival (#5994), long transcript lines are assembled once on import (#5981), OpenCode token usage is streamed (#5974), and legacy prompt retry lookups in the viewer are bounded by time (#6011).

Docs

  • Agent framework guides are grouped, and a setting that does not exist was removed (#6030).
  • The token claim is stated as a per-result size ratio (#5889, thanks @Zhong-fan, #4138).

Full changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v15.17.1...v15.18.0

v15.17.1 Stable2026-11-11T18:22:35Z

Open sessions keep capturing after an update

Since 15.16.0 the worker clears old plugin versions out of Claude Code's plugin cache five minutes after it starts and then hourly. A Claude Code session that was already open when an update arrived keeps running from the older version folder. Once the sweep deleted that folder, Claude Code refused to run every claude-mem hook for that session ("Plugin directory does not exist"), so the session silently stopped saving prompts, tool calls, observations and summaries.

The sweep now reads the .in_use records Claude Code keeps for each plugin version, and keeps a version while any session loaded from it is still running. Folders no running session uses are still removed on the next sweep, so the disk clean-up from 15.16.0 stays. The worker's sweep, npx claude-mem prune (and its --dry-run) and the installer all follow the same rule (#5971, thanks @rigel905, fixes #5970).

Fixes

  • Context: an explicitly empty project selection shows nothing instead of observations from every project (#5866, thanks @Afloat16).
  • Context: read-cost and savings estimates count stored facts once, instead of counting extra escape characters and an invented [] for rows without facts (#5980, thanks @Afloat16).
  • Observer: a streamed answer that arrives as a refusal counts as generated output, so an interrupted stream is not sent again and charged twice (#5843, thanks @Afloat16).
  • My Work: the API agent rejects an answer the provider cut with content_filter, instead of saving an incomplete report (#5878, thanks @Afloat16).
  • Sync: a pulled page of synced records waits for another writer instead of failing at once with "database is locked", so a large first pull no longer backs off for minutes (#5996).
  • My Work: the worker reads My Work's shared history once per change instead of re-reading all of it on every sync pass (#5986).
  • Cloud viewer: a slow-starting cloud viewer reloads its "starting" page until the worker answers, and a hidden cloud My Work tab closes its live feed until it is shown again (#5988, #5953).

Integrations

New verified memory workflows for agent frameworks, each scoped to one project: Vercel AI SDK (#5951), LangGraph (#5954), Pydantic AI (#5955), Microsoft Agent Framework (#6017), Haystack (#6018), LlamaIndex (#6019), Google ADK (#6020), CrewAI (#6021), Strands Agents (#6023), OpenAI Agents SDK (#6024), Agno (#6025), SmolAgents (#6026), AG2 (#6027) and AutoGen (#6028). Thanks @Afloat16 for all of them.

Also in this release

  • Cloud viewer: groundwork for routing each signed-in account to its own app; production behaviour is unchanged (#5952).
  • eve template: the template's lockfile includes claude-mem 15.17.0, so its deploy button installs (#5968).

Full changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v15.17.0...v15.17.1

First-time preview setup

Existing stable versions do not support --channel. Download the archive for your OS and architecture from this release, verify it against SHA256SUMS, and extract the entire archive into its own directory (keep all Linux companion files together). Run the extracted binary explicitly, using its full path, rather than the old stable jcode on PATH. For Windows, use the extracted .exe.

From that preview binary, run jcode update --channel preview (substitute its full path for jcode) to persist the opt-in channel and receive future previews. To roll back, run the preview binary with update --channel stable, or reinstall the current stable release. Stable users are never moved to previews automatically.

This preview series ends when the v1.0.0 stable tag is created.

jgm/pandoc ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
3.14pandoc 3.14 Stable2026-11-12T04:32:26Z

I'm pleased to announce the release of pandoc 3.14, available in the usual places:

Binary packages & changelog: https://github.com/jgm/pandoc/releases/tag/3.14

Source & API documentation: http://hackage.haskell.org/package/pandoc-3.14

This release includes a lot of bug fixes and small improvements, including improvements in dependencies, which you can read about in the changelog. Here are the changes of special note:

  • Pandoc now searches a path given in an enviornment variable PANDOC_DATA_DIRS when looking up data files (including templates, filters, defaults files, metadata files, etc.). The user data directory is still tried first. This change will make it easier to create distributions of pandoc with special customized data files.

  • The LaTeX writer now uses custom macros for footnotes in tables and definition lists, fixing a bug with hyperlinks in the notes in these contexts. Those who use custom templates will need to import the definitions of these macros from the default LaTeX template.

API change:

  • Text.Pandoc.Class exports new functions getDataDirs and getPandocDataDirs.

Thanks to all who contributed, especially new contributors Alain Lesage, Asuka, Caio Vinícius, Jeremy Levartovsky, Nacho García, tyler000000.

<details> <summary>Click to expand changelog</summary>

  • Support PANDOC_DATA_DIRS for data file lookup (#7922). If PANDOC_DATA_DIRS is set, the (absolute) directories it lists are searched, in order, for data files not found in the user data directory, before falling back to the default data files. This covers templates, filters, defaults files, metadata files, CSL styles, and other data files. If PANDOC_DATA_DIRS is set, print its value in --version output.

  • Reset sourcepos when parsing footnotes and grid table cells (#12001, extends fix for #11888). Otherwise, when one of these ends a file, relative paths in it can be rebased against the next file on the command line.

  • LaTeX reader:

    • Fix duplicated text after a control space at line end (#12051, Asuka).
    • Handle pandoc’s deferred footnotes (\pandocfootnotemark{n} and \pandocfootnotetext{n}{..}) as produced by the LaTeX writer for notes in tables, captions, etc.
    • Ignore definitions of \pandocbounded, \pandocfootnotemark, and \pandocfootnotetext (e.g. from pandoc’s template), which the reader handles directly. Previously these definitions were expanded, producing garbage and losing content.
    • Fix file-crossing bug in rawLaTeXParser (#12072). The raw LaTeX parser didn’t work properly when crossing from one source file to another.

  • Typst reader:

    • Handle columns: auto in grid (#12061).

  • HTML reader:

    • Don’t drop ordered lists and rules inside footnotes (#11852). Previously an <ol> inside the body of a note was treated as the list of notes, and its items were lost. Similarly, an <hr> inside a note was dropped.

  • RST reader:

    • Strip minimum indentation from indented blocks (#12071). Previously the indentation of the first line was stripped, so lines with less indentation lost all their leading whitespace. Now, as in docutils, the minimum indentation is stripped and relative indentation is preserved.
    • Require descriptions in option list items (#12063).

  • ODT reader:

    • Support the styles extension (#12060).

  • Docx reader:

    • Merge adjacent styled code blocks (#9183, Caio Vinícius). Without +styles, consecutive “Source Code” paragraphs are combined into a single code block by combineBlocks. With +styles, each paragraph is first wrapped in a Div carrying its custom-style attribute, so the CodeBlock case no longer matched and every line became its own Div [CodeBlock].
    • Don’t drop contents of customXml element (#12050).
    • Read all underline types (#11988, Patrick Creighton).
    • Follow w:numStyleLink in abstract numberings (#9222, Caio Vinícius).

  • Pptx reader:

    • Don’t add spaces between text runs (#12016, Nacho García).
    • Read the shapes inside group shapes (#12017, Nacho García).
    • Read equations and markup-compatibility blocks (#12025, Nacho Garcia).
    • Read speaker notes (Nacho García, #12023). Each slide’s notes are now read into a notes div at the end of the slide’s blocks, the form the pptx writer turns into speaker notes, so pptx -> Markdown -> pptx keeps them.
    • Read charts as tables (Nacho García, #12023). Previously, a chart was read as a placeholder text. We now create a table from the chart’s cached data. The table is put in a chart Div whose type attribute holds the chart type. The chart’s title becomes the table captions. The chart code is in Text.Pandoc.Readers.OOXML.Charts.
    • Mark hidden slides with a hidden class on the header (Nacho García, #12023).
    • Handle merged table cells (Nacho García).
    • Parse bold, italic, sub/superscript and hyperlinks (Nacho García). Text runs were previously read as plain text, so their formatting and hyperlinks were lost.
    • Read inherited bullets, numbering and nested lists (#12042, Nacho García).
    • Skip slide number placeholders (#12045, Nacho García).

  • Xlsx reader:

    • Resolve package-absolute relationship targets (#12013, Nacho García).
    • Read inline strings and rich text as written (#12014, Nacho García).
    • Look up a cell’s font through its cell format (#12033, Nacho García).
    • Read merged cells as spans (Nacho García).
    • Render dates and times (Nacho García).
    • Render numbers as Excel’s General format (Nacho García, #12023).
    • Read charts (#12043, Nacho García). Charts are now read as tables using the cached data.

  • Docx writer:

    • Implement flip-flopping for emph (#11961). Emph within emph becomes upright. This is important for correct formatting of titles in CSL, among other things. LaTeX does this automatically, and in HTML we achieve it via CSS.

  • EPUB writer:

    • Fix id clash with added title header (#12003, Alain Lesage).
    • Skip dates that cannot be normalized (#12002, Alain Lesage).

  • Ipynb writer:

    • Make ANSI escape stripping linear (#7744, Jeremy Levartovsky).

  • Vimwiki reader:

    • Support rejected todos (tyler000000).

  • LaTeX writer:

    • Fix hyperlinks for deferred footnotes (#11936). Notes in tables, captions, and definition list terms are rendered as a mark followed later by the note text. Previously we used \footnotemark{} and \footnotetext with manual adjustments to the footnote counter. But hyperref anchors each \footnotetext at the destination of the most recent \footnotemark, so when there were several such notes, all of the texts got the last mark’s anchor and the links for the other marks were broken. We now emit \pandocfootnotemark{n} and \pandocfootnotetext{n}{..}, where n is a unique id. These macros, defined in the default template when the deferred-notes variable is set, save the footnote mark and hyperref anchor at the mark and restore them at the text. This removes the need for counter arithmetic, works when the footnote counter is reset by chapter, and handles notes nested inside deferred notes (whose texts are now emitted after the text containing their marks). Note: Users of custom LaTeX templates, or of LaTeX fragments produced without --standalone, will need to include the definitions of \pandocfootnotemark and \pandocfootnotetext from the default template.
    • Allow repeated longtable captions (#4558, Václav Haisman). If a table has a repeat-caption class, captions will be repeated on each page of a longtable.

  • HTML styles template:

    • Add emph flip-flopping. So, emph within emph renders as upright, etc.
    • Move @print rules to end (#12064), so they aren’t overridden.

  • Text.Pandoc.PDF:

    • PDF via LaTeX: do at most 3 runs when only a TOC is present (#12012, Alain Lesage).

  • Text.Pandoc.Class:

    • Export new functions getDataDirs and getPandocDataDirs. [API change]

  • Text.Pandoc.Citeproc:

    • removeFinalPeriod now acts across Link boundary.

  • pandoc-lua-engine: raise lower bound for pandoc to 3.13 (#12066).

  • Depend on texmath 0.13.3.5, commonmark 0.3.2, commonmark-extensions 0.2.8, citeproc 0.14.2, typst 0.12.1, emojis 0.1.6, skylighting 0.15.0.1.

  • New bounds for crypton in pandoc-lua-engine and pandoc.

  • Makefile:

    • Fix release-templates target so it pushes tags.
    • Remove default CABALOPTS. These can be put in cabal.project.local. This will ensure that the same options are used by HLS, so that rebuilds aren’t needed.
    • make check-cabal: exit with error status on outdated deps.
    • Reorganize check-cabal; add check-sdist target we can run separately.

  • cabal.project: specify +http flag for all ghc versions.

  • Minimize the test docx, odt, pptx files. This reduces the size of the test files (and release tarball) by 1.79 MB.

</details>

View release →

can1357/oh-my-pi ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v18.19.0 Stable2026-11-12T03:35:49Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Added

  • Added the liquid-systemone judgment API for Liquid AI decision models, including validation that rejects image sets exceeding Liquid’s 10,000 32×32-patch request limit before submission.

Fixed

  • Fixed Gemini CLI and Antigravity responses losing or misordering thought signatures and reply text, including responses that follow tool calls.
  • Fixed Gemini CLI and Antigravity merging reply text around a thought signature, dropping signatures at the start of a reply or after a tool call, and placing a thought ahead of reply text that streamed before it (#18942 by @JustasMasiulis)
  • Fixed Google Antigravity judging Gemini models by quota buckets they do not consume: Gemini 3.x requests ignore the Gemini 4 Argon weekly bucket, and Argon requests ignore gemini-weekly, so an exhausted or low bucket no longer blocks, reserves, or caps an account for the other models (#16104).

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Added

  • Added Liquid AI provider support with the d1.1 and d1-ultra judge models and Liquid authentication via /login liquid using LIQUID_API_KEY.

Fixed

  • Fixed Google Antigravity Gemini 4 Argon models sharing the general Gemini quota scope instead of their dedicated weekly bucket (#16104).

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Breaking Changes

  • Changed the browser open API: ignore_https_errors is now insecure, allow_file_access has been removed, and file: pages can access other local files.
  • Changed the browser open and targets app option from an object to a string or CDP endpoint/executable path. Use argv instead of app.args and the top-level target instead of app.target.
  • Removed task.file and the task tool's taskFile field; subagent instructions must now be provided inline.
  • Removed blocked todo tasks and the blocked status/blocker metadata. Existing blocked tasks are restored as pending.

Added

  • Added systemPromptTemplates, which maps model-selector patterns to Handlebars template files for customizing system prompts. Templates re-render when switching models, with warnings and fallback to the bundled prompt for missing or invalid templates.
  • Added ordered multi-step tasks through task.steps. Subagents receive steps sequentially, and the final step determines the task result; interrupted tasks resume with their remaining steps.
  • Added Liquid AI judge models liquid/d1.1 and liquid/d1-ultra, including image resizing for Liquid's request limits.
  • Added per-step progress, state, and duration reporting for GitHub Actions watches, with expandable details and native Tern Surface Protocol views.
  • Added task.autoParallel (off, suggest, or on) to identify subtasks that can run concurrently and optionally delegate them in a single parallel task call.
  • Added portable awk, tar, lsof, shasum, md5, and strings support to the bash tool, including Windows support and internal-URI paths.

Changed

  • Browser sessions now prefer the bundled ABKit WebKit engine when available, with automatic installation and SHA-256 verification. WebKit provides headed, user-visible tabs and broader browser compatibility; Chromium remains the fallback and can be selected explicitly with app: "chromium", while app: "webkit" requires WebKit. Configure this behavior with browser.webkit and browser.webkitPath or the corresponding environment variables.
  • Browser visibility controls now show or hide WebKit tab windows immediately. Headed WebKit tabs can be interacted with directly, while Chromium tabs retain their existing mode-switching behavior.
  • In Tern environments with native DOM access, browser observation and interaction use trusted native input across frames and closed shadow roots, with caret-aware typing and case-insensitive text selectors.
  • edit.modelVariants now uses the longest matching model pattern, so specific patterns take precedence over generic ones regardless of configuration order.
  • The ask tool now submits the form's current values when its timeout expires; updated settings and documentation reflect this behavior.
  • The read tool loads detailed format selectors and URI sub-path syntax on demand, reducing the default request description while retaining inline details when read is disabled.
  • The default system prompt and tool descriptions have been streamlined, with guidance moved closer to the tools and detailed task/procedure references provided when relevant.
  • The bash pseudo view renders words built from literals and expansions as f-strings (f"repos/org/{r}/rulesets") instead of concat(...) calls.

Fixed

  • Fixed computer.close() so subsequent computer-control calls start a fresh desktop session instead of failing.
  • Fixed extension providers with oauth.modifyModels so they do not load the full model catalog before login.
  • Fixed resuming sessions so it no longer scans unrelated later session transcripts.
  • Fixed session saving on Windows to avoid UI stalls caused by repeated session-file access.
  • Fixed automatic chart selection so generated charts use compatible data and chart types.
  • Fixed bash previews for env -i commands so they display the requested command rather than generated shell setup code.

@oh-my-pi/collab-web

Fixed

  • Fixed task tool cards so spawned tasks consistently display their name, assigned agent, and instructions, with steps spawns shown as numbered stages.

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Added

  • Added shellBuiltins(sessionEnv?) to list commands provided by the embedded shell, including process controls and utilities.
  • Expanded the embedded shell with awk, tar, shasum, md5, strings, and lsof across Linux, macOS, and Windows. Destructive utilities can be disabled with PI_DISABLE_UUTILS_DESTRUCTIVE; when disabled, tar is delegated to the system.

Fixed

  • Fixed macOS text entry in Chrome and other Chromium-based applications so typing followed by Return opens the entered text instead of reloading the current page.

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Added

  • GitHub Actions watch cards now show current-step progress and can be expanded with Ctrl+O to display ordered step states and durations in ANSI and native Tern views.

Fixed

  • Fixed comparison charts mislabeling gaps against a rival column as improvements or regressions.
  • Fixed Tern edit diffs showing unrelated one-line hunks and incorrect line numbers around distant changes.
  • Fixed table charts incorrectly interpreting words such as “with,” “to,” and “from” in column names as comparison conditions.
  • Fixed chart row names containing trailing numbers, such as “EmbeddingGemma 2” and “Jev 15,” being split or incorrectly nested.
  • Fixed read rejecting colon-separated line ranges and displaying a single requested range as multiple rows.

Removed

  • Removed the blocked status from TodoStatus, the blocker field from TodoItem, and the block/unblock operations from TodoOperation.

@oh-my-pi/pi-utils

Added

  • Added browser utilities exposing trusted browser archive extraction limits and the installed WebKit host executable path.

What's Changed

  • feat(ai,catalog,coding-agent): add Liquid AI (d1-ultra, d1.1) judge provider by @SuperUserA in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18100
  • fix(collab): isolate test home directories and guard real ~/.omp paths by @iliaal in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18811
  • fix(ai): keep signature-only thought parts as signed thinking on Gemini CLI routes by @JustasMasiulis in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18942
  • perf(tui): release regenerable render caches on retired tool cards by @iliaal in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18820
  • perf(coding-agent,catalog): keep model cache reads provider-scoped and prune expired legacy rows by @iliaal in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18821
  • feat(coding-agent): add per-model system prompt templates by @DeprecatedLuke in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/17835
  • perf(session): read only headers when looking for a session's continuations by @iliaal in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18952
  • feat(github): show step-level Actions watch progress by @anatoli-tsinovoy in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18945
  • fix(ai): isolate antigravity argon quota bucket by @roboomp in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/16106

v18.18.9 Stable2026-11-11T19:06:16Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-agent-core

Added

  • Tool calls get ctx.toolCall.earlierEffectsSettled, which settles once the earlier calls of their message that run beside them and may write files (their approval, asked with their arguments, is not read) have finished; speculative results wait for those effects before validation, and cancellation while waiting prevents their commit (#16274 by @will-bogusz)

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Added

  • Added SOFYA_API_KEY as the env key for the sofya provider (#18185 by @yusufgurdogan)

Fixed

  • Devin requests no longer send temperature: 0: the catalog's temperature-floor raises it to 0.0001, so session titles and Mnemopi memory extraction work on Devin instead of failing with Cascade's opaque invalid_argument and silently falling back to another model (#18423)
  • Auth-gateway /v1/messages now answers 400 invalid_request_error for output_config.format (and the pre-GA output_format) instead of returning 200 with output the schema never constrained, so structured-output clients fall back to prompt-side schemas (#18842)
  • Auth-gateway requests denied only the requested model (Codex ChatGPT account, Cursor plan) now switch to another account and keep the denied one usable for other models, instead of marking a working credential suspect; gateway rotation now follows the same rules as local sessions, including waiting out a sibling's short usage block

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Added

  • Added the web/sofya search model (#18185 by @yusufgurdogan)
  • Added the temperature-floor compat axis, assigned 0.0001 on Devin so a request that pins temperature: 0 reaches Cascade as a value it accepts (#18423)
  • Added the keyless web/hackernews search engine model for Hacker News stories and comments

Fixed

  • Devin requests now identify as Devin CLI 3000.12.43, the current stable release, instead of 3000.11.205 (#18766 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Gemini 4 Argon and Carbon on Command Code showing no reasoning levels; both now offer low, medium, and high
  • Fixed OpenCode Zen Gemini 4 Carbon requests going to the chat-completions endpoint instead of the native Gemini endpoint Zen serves it on

Removed

  • Removed Devin's retired SWE-1.6 Fast from the models omp lists before Devin's live model list loads (first start, or when Devin is unreachable) (#18795 by @will-bogusz)

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Added

  • Added Sofya as a web search engine (web/sofya), using SOFYA_API_KEY or a stored sofya credential (#18185 by @yusufgurdogan)
  • Documented autoresearch mode in docs/autoresearch.md: activation and its git gates, the three stop conditions, the per-segment iteration cap that defaults to none, the harness METRIC/ASI contract, and the on-disk store that OMP_AUTORESEARCH_DB_DIR relocates (#18655 by @sjawhar)
  • Extensions can import the form theme, the native span helpers (styledSpans, compactText, rowsText and the rest of the ANSI/span conversions), the xd:// URL helpers (parseXdUrl and friends) and the cfgExtensionTranscriptPresenters setting handle from @oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent instead of internal module paths, which stop the extension from loading when a file moves (#18849 by @materemias)
  • /context shows loaded instruction files and skills in their own categories; System Prompt marks where each one sits without counting it twice, and notes when an extension or SDK callback changed the prompt after it was built (#17971 by @c-w-z)
  • Added Hacker News search (web/hackernews): web_search queries scoped to site:news.ycombinator.com now search HN stories and comments through the keyless HN Search API before the configured engines

Changed

  • A role edit in another omp instance no longer rewrites the task and eval tool descriptions of every running session, which was invalidating the Anthropic prompt cache from the tool definitions onward and dropping signed thinking blocks on prefix-bound models; the per-call model advertisement is now frozen into the base prompt surface and changes only when that surface rebuilds (#18716, #18732 by @yuzu-octopus)
  • Agent-to-agent messages now always reach the recipient as user turns: one that ends a wait arrives right after it instead of inside the wait tool result, and none is sent with developer or system authority
  • Launch services now persist for non‑interactive sessions
  • The shell's built-in jq now behaves like jq 1.8.2 instead of jaq: the same output (number formatting, colors, -S, --indent, --seq, --stream), error messages and exit statuses, jq's full option set (--version reports jq-1.8.2), and its grammar and builtins (?//, $__loc__, modules, getpath/setpath creating missing paths, |= deletions, Oniguruma regexes, dates); it passes jq 1.8.2's own test suite

Fixed

  • Nix builds with withAppleFm = false no longer compile the Apple Foundation Models bridge with the host's Xcode (#18911)
  • Apple Silicon Nix builds include Apple Foundation Models support instead of silently building without it (#16584 by @honsunrise)
  • On Windows, the bash tool runs external commands when omp itself runs in an AppContainer sandbox, instead of failing every one with Permission denied (exit 126) (#18800 by @devjay4561)
  • Explicit Bash prompt rules remain enforced in yolo mode when a command also matches a critical pattern (#18788 by @jasonharrison).
  • Fixed job widgets and captions failing outright when the widget model errors: they now try the role's next model (retry.fallbackChains.widget, else the built-in smol defaults) and fail only once every candidate has (#18793)
  • Failed job-widget, job-caption, cache-warm, composer-prediction, and subagent completion-probe calls now keep the provider's error message in their session usage records (#18792)
  • /changelog, /changelog full, and /changelog last N now list releases newest-first, matching CHANGELOG.md and the startup release notes (#18809)
  • Fixed a read placed after a bash call in the same message returning the file as it was before that command changed, created or deleted it, in primary and advisor turns (#16274 by @will-bogusz)
  • RPC/ACP /session delete now switches the live session before deleting its file, so later turns save to a new, resumable session (#18665).
  • Browser tab.click, tab.dblclick, tab.check, tab.uncheck, tab.uploadFile and frame.click on Chromium tabs now look their selector up again when the page replaces the element before the click goes out, including during and after delivery watcher setup, instead of waiting out the action timeout on the replaced element; a click through an element handle or an aria-ref whose element was replaced fails at once (#18676 by @will-bogusz)
  • A browser tab.run whose code uses document, window, location or another page-only global now says the code runs in omp's runtime, not the page, and to use tab.evaluate, instead of only "document is not defined" (#18840 by @will-bogusz)
  • JavaScript eval cells and tab.run/computer.run strings ending in assignments, blocks, loops, or declarations now return nothing; trailing expressions and explicit returns still produce values (#18865 by @will-bogusz)
  • A page calling window.print() no longer hangs the browser omp launches: print() returns at once and tab.pdf() still makes PDFs (#18838 by @will-bogusz)
  • tab.select() and element-handle select() return the options selected after the page's input and change handlers ran, instead of the choice the page then reset (Chromium tabs) or an option the page's handler had just replaced (cmux and Tern tabs) (#18829 by @will-bogusz)
  • tab.waitForDownload() fails, naming the path it looked for, when no file is there on omp's machine, as with a browser reached through app.cdp_url on another machine, instead of returning a path that does not exist; such a download is left out of tab.downloads() (#18830 by @will-bogusz)
  • When Chrome detaches omp from a relay tab omp opened and omp cannot reattach, the error now says omp tried to close that tab and to open a new one, instead of telling the agent to navigate it in Chrome; a failed close may be deferred until the relay reconnects (#18832 by @will-bogusz)
  • A relay app.target that matches no tab now recommends omitting app.target to open omp's own tab (#18833 by @will-bogusz)
  • Judgments skip a judge candidate for five minutes after it answers 404 for its model, as they already do after a 401/402/403, instead of re-sending the failing request on every judgment of a find call (#18854)
  • Fixed Tern and other OSC 7501 terminals showing a session as working, with no needs-you alert, while a dialog waits on you, such as a ctx.ui.confirm an extension's tool_call handler opens to gate a command: every dialog the TUI shows (extension and hook dialogs, tool approvals, ask, cfg:// changes, the coding-plan reserve confirm, plan review) now reports blocked with what it asks until you answer, and a tool approval reports working again as soon as you answer it (#15107, #18868 by @wolfiesch)
  • The shell's built-in jq no longer slows down quadratically on repeated updates such as reduce range(n) as $i ([]; . + [$i])
  • /tan completion notices now quote the user's request and mark the result as the user's tangent, so the main agent no longer mistakes it for its own work
  • omp zsh with zsh-autosuggestions' completion strategy no longer echoes each typed key onto new lines: vared inside a zpty command now edits on the pty instead of the shell's terminal
  • An instruction file that leaves a code fence open has it closed at its end, so the rest of the system prompt is formatted as usual (#17971 by @c-w-z)
  • Read-only activity stays visible after snap-compaction or another mid-turn transcript rebuild instead of leaving the UI stuck on “Working…” (#18876 by @JustasMasiulis).
  • With the Spotlight experiment on, a session now warns when Spotlight finds no Stencil credential, once each time the credential goes missing, and says how to sign in again; the spotlight:shape journal entry records failed Spotlight calls by cause (failureCauses) (#18850 by @wolfiesch)

Removed

  • Removed /mcp smithery-search, /mcp smithery-login and /mcp smithery-logout; Smithery shut down its API on October 31, 2026 (#18822 by @samettemizer)

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Added

  • OMP_APPLEFM_DISABLE=1 builds without the Apple Foundation Models bridge and never probes Xcode or the Command Line Tools; it cannot be combined with OMP_APPLEFM_SWIFTC (#18911)

Changed

  • The macOS addon's install name is @rpath/libpi_natives.dylib instead of the directory it was built in (#18911)
  • A set OMP_APPLEFM_SWIFTC must name a usable Swift 6.4+ compiler with a macOS 27+ SDK: the build now fails instead of falling back to Xcode, the Command Line Tools, or a build without Apple Foundation Models (#16584 by @honsunrise)

Fixed

  • Fixed macOS window screenshots drawing the window smaller than it appears, so clicks aimed from them missed by tens of points; windows spanning displays or partly off screen are captured whole at their on-screen size, except while a sheet larger than the window is open (#17757 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Linux computer reads crashing the app being read (Qt 5 and Qt 6 before 6.9.2 apps anywhere; GTK 3 and Firefox apps on distributions with at-spi2-core before 2.45, such as Ubuntu 22.04), and reading no text from the text fields of newer Qt apps (#18823 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed macOS background left clicks crashing the page in Electron apps, and in Chrome when the click changes the URL in place (history.pushState, as single-page apps do) (#18824 by @will-bogusz)

@oh-my-pi/omp-stats

Added

  • The Spotlight dashboard's Overhead card breaks failed results down by cause (not signed in, timeout, rate limited, HTTP status, …) and, when results failed for lack of a Stencil sign-in, says how to sign in (#18850 by @wolfiesch)

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Added

  • Added the Sofya label for sofya web search results (#18185 by @yusufgurdogan)
  • The context breakdown counts instruction files and skills from the system prompt as built, matching /context, and lists Instruction files as its own category (#17971 by @c-w-z)
  • Context Viewer adds an Instruction Files category and opens Skills like any other category; ContextViewEntry.sourceMarkers colors viewer-only source markers by category in previews and full content, and ContextViewData.notices lists snapshot caveats under the Overview legend (#17971 by @c-w-z)

Fixed

  • In Tern, an ask question outside a <fieldset> gets its own tab instead of sharing the tab of the question before it (#18660 by @DocksDocks)
  • The /resume picker shows dates of sessions older than a week in the locale from LC_ALL, LC_TIME or LANG instead of always using MM/DD/YYYY (#18863 by @raoulkent)
  • Fixed an ask HTML form in Tern not taking typing after a click on its Other row or a text well while a composer draft held the keyboard: the click now hands the keys to the form, as ↑/↓ does, instead of every key still editing the draft
  • Agent Hub status scopes (All, Running, Done, Parked) show icons in Tern instead of empty squares (#18880 by @a-glapinski)
  • Fixed streaming tables that overflow the screen staying squeezed to their header's column widths after the rows arrive (#18909 by @JustasMasiulis)

Removed

  • Removed estimateSkillsTokens() and the skillful context-usage option; Skills tokens come from the built prompt's sources (#17971 by @c-w-z)

@oh-my-pi/pi-utils

Added

  • Added prompt.renderTracked: renders a template like prompt.render and reports where each {{#each}} iteration over a tracked item lands in the output (#17971 by @c-w-z)
  • Added prompt.closeCodeFence: closes a code fence a text leaves open, by the same rule prompt.format uses (#17971 by @c-w-z)

Release candidate for 1.0.3. Install it explicitly: pip install 'browser-use==1.0.3rc1'. Plain installs still get 1.0.2.

  • --cli-mcp exits cleanly when the client closed the pipe before the reply.
  • The CLI runs its own Chromium when no browser has remote debugging on or there is no display.
  • A BU_CDP_URL nothing listens on fails in 3 s with one clear message.

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v4.0.30CC Switch v4.0.30 Stable2026-11-12T01:36:15Z

CC Switch v4.0.30

本版本新增隐私过滤器：在路由和聚合模式下，本地路由转发请求前可以把用户输入和系统提示里的个人信息和密钥替换成占位符，不让它们发到上游。默认关闭，开关在「设置 → 数据」。

另外修复了 Codex 经路由连第三方原生 Responses 上游时，请求带上只有 OpenAI 认的字段被整条拒绝（报 400）；工具调用参数里出现重复的键后，Codex 对话每一轮都报 400；Windows 上主窗口打开在任务栏下面、按钮点不到；Antigravity 免费账号查额度报 403、锁区账号看不出原因；Antigravity 用量同步每分钟重读全部会话占满 CPU 等问题。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

隐私过滤器

开启后，本地路由在转发请求前，会把用户输入和系统提示中的个人信息和密钥替换为 [邮箱]、[密钥] 这样的占位符（#8951）。开关在「设置 → 数据 → 隐私过滤器」，默认关闭。

  • 会替换什么：邮箱、中国大陆手机号、身份证号、银行卡号、IPv4 地址（127.0.0.1 这类回环地址除外），以及 API Key、Token、私钥、「密码是…」「api_key=…」这类口令。
  • 不会动什么：模型回复、思考内容、工具调用参数和图片。
  • 工具结果（文件内容、命令输出等）默认不处理，可以用「同时过滤工具结果」子开关打开。打开后模型只能看到占位符，改文件时可能因为对不上原文而失败，整文件重写时占位符可能被写回你的文件，请确认能接受再开。
  • 只在路由和聚合模式下生效。直连模式下请求不经过 CC Switch，不会被过滤。
  • 完全在本机按规则匹配，不调用任何模型；替换不可逆，占位符跟随界面语言（韩语、俄语界面暂用英文占位符）。规则移植自 packyme/privacy-filter。

新模型价格

  • 内置 Claude Fable 5.2（#9426）和 Gemini 4 Carbon（#9421，按截至 11 月 30 日的介绍价）的价格，用量统计里不再显示「未定价」。已经自己填过价格的不会被覆盖。

变更

  • 打开供应商编辑页更快（#9357）：以前重启后第一次点编辑要等 1~2 秒，供应商多时打开、关闭编辑页也会卡一下，在名称框打字也不够跟手。现在启动后会在空闲时预先加载编辑页，打开、关闭和输入都不再拖慢整个列表。
  • Codex OAuth 卡片显示账号（#9300）：Claude Code 和 Claude Desktop 里的 Codex OAuth 供应商卡片，副标题从 ChatGPT 价格页网址改为「账号 · 套餐」，和其他官方账号卡片一致。
  • Copilot 官方登录卡片并入「已添加」：不再单独占一组，排在已添加供应商的第一个。
  • 手动改过 SKILL.md 后，Skills 页面的名称和描述会更新（#9316，相关 #7848）：在后台检查，不再在打开 Skills 页面、切换开关时扫描全部技能目录。

修复

  • Codex 经路由连第三方原生 Responses 上游时，请求被整条拒绝，报 400（#9042、#9077）。v4.0.28 起，有 ChatGPT 登录时第三方路由也写成 Codex 内置的 openai，Codex 因此会带上只有 OpenAI 自己认的字段，LM Studio、LiteLLM 和部分中转这类校验严格的上游会拒绝整个请求。现在发往第三方时按 Codex 自己对非 OpenAI 上游的处理把这些字段都去掉；官方卡和授权中心的 ChatGPT 账号不受影响。
  • 工具调用参数里出现重复的键后，Codex 对话每一轮都报 400，再也继续不了（#9339、#9341）。模型偶尔会在一次工具调用里把同一个参数写两遍，Codex 每一轮都会把历史原样再发一遍，校验严格的上游就一直拒绝。现在发往第三方原生 Responses 上游前，重复的键只保留最后一个；官方不受影响。
  • Windows 上主窗口打开时被任务栏挡住下半截，更新弹窗的按钮点不到，重启也一样（#9206、#9336）。现在启动时会把窗口挪回屏幕可用区域内，太大就缩小；macOS 上被程序坞挡住同理。
  • Antigravity 免费账号查额度报「403 You do not have a valid license of this product」；锁区账号在授权中心照常显示额度，看不出为什么登录不了（#9413、#9414）。现在查额度的方式与 agy 一致；Google 判定账号不可用（例如地区不支持）时，显示 Google 给出的原因。
  • agy 升级到 1.5 后，授权中心认不出正在用的 Antigravity 账号；切换账号后，没绑定账号的 Antigravity 官方卡片显示的还是上一个号的额度（#9318、#9320）。
  • Antigravity 会话多时，CC Switch 在后台持续占满一个 CPU 核、大量读盘（#9346）。以前每分钟把所有会话记录完整读两遍，现在没变化的会话直接跳过。
  • 经路由使用 Gemini 时，流式请求的用量记成请求里的模型名，不是实际回答的模型；Vertex AI 形式的上游成功请求在用量统计里没有记录（#7738、#7740）。
  • Copilot 自带 Key 的供应商，填了「上下文窗口」和「最大输出 Token」、没填「最大输入 Token」的模型，输入仍被限制在 128K：现在按「上下文窗口 − 最大输出 Token」写入最大输入，自己填过的值不变。
  • Windows 上没装 Copilot CLI、但装了 VS Code 时，Copilot CLI 显示为「已安装·无法运行」：VS Code 自带的 Copilot 占位程序不再被当成 Copilot CLI，现在正确显示为未安装。
  • macOS 和 Linux 上升级后弹出的更新摘要被压扁，只露出一行（#9401）。
  • OpenCode 的 Oh My OpenCode Slim 表单所有按钮点了都没反应（#2067、#3905）。
  • v3.7.0~v3.8.0 发布说明底部的 README / CHANGELOG 链接打不开（#7744、#7750）。

stablyai/orca ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.4.2571.4.257 Stable2026-11-12T01:20:37Z

Previous release: 1.4.256 — Easier on-device dictation setup and earlier long-chat performance improvements.

Smoother long chats, saved feedback and better dictation ✨

Orca 1.4.257 reduces freezes when several long Chat UI conversations stream at once. If you keep multiple agents working in conversations with a lot of history, this update cuts the work Orca does as their replies arrive.

  • 🌐 Keep your browser feedback across restarts: Unsent text notes on pages in Orca’s browser now return after a reload or restart. Closing a tab with unsent notes asks before discarding them, so you can leave feedback and come back to it later. Agents running on your desktop can read those notes with orca tab annotations before you press Send.
  • 🎙️ Give yourself time to think while dictating: In Settings → Voice → Input → Stop After Silence, choose 30 seconds, one minute, two minutes or Off. Dictation can wait through a longer pause while you compose a message or terminal command. The same settings page adds a Test button to record and play back your microphone, with a transcript when your speech model is ready.
  • ⌨️ Paste while a terminal connects: A command pasted into a starting or reconnecting terminal now waits for its shell instead of being dropped. This covers local, SSH and remote terminals for pastes up to 64 KB; larger pastes still need a connected terminal.
  • 🗂️ Automate folder workspace routines: Configured setup scripts now run when you create a folder workspace, including through the CLI. Archive scripts run before removal. Use them to prepare the project and run its cleanup routine; these scripts operate in the shared project folder.

Highlights

  • Find what belongs in a split pane: Press / in an empty split pane to search for an agent or file. Use the arrow keys and Enter to open your selection without leaving the keyboard.
  • Keep your chosen Claude model: The Chat UI picker keeps your latest selection when replies from an earlier turn arrive, rather than snapping back to their model.
  • See the images you asked Codex to make: Generated images remain visible after a turn finishes, without expanding its tool details. For remote chats, update both the host and client to get this behavior.

Highlights

Fixes

  • Security: reject malformed URI identifiers using fast-uri 4.2.3 while preserving valid relative references. (#187284) Thanks @RomneyDa and @shakkernerd.

  • Security: reject oversized bounded collections early in the Discord SDK and its transitive Zod 3 parser without restoring the retired A2UI integration. (#187296 #187303) Thanks @RomneyDa and @shakkernerd.

(#188527 #189869 #190982; related #189845, #190980) Authored by @steipete. Thanks @RomneyDa, @shakkernerd, and @Satoshi-Kusumoto.

Fission-AI/OpenSpec ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2.0.0v2.0.0 - Skills in .agents/skills, /opsx commands removed Stable2026-11-11T22:41:44Z

OpenSpec 2.0

OpenSpec now installs skills in .agents/skills/ instead of keeping a separate copy for each coding agent. Tools that don't read this folder, like Claude Code and Kiro, get links to the same skills.

We're also removing the /opsx:* slash commands. Each command already has a matching skill, so there's no need to maintain both.

The OpenSpec workflows themselves haven't changed.

What changed

  • Skills are installed in .agents/skills/. Every tool uses the same skills, without duplicate files across folders.
  • /opsx:* commands are removed. For example, /opsx:propose becomes /openspec-propose (or $openspec-propose in Codex). You can also just ask your agent for the work.
  • openspec update removes files generated by older versions. This includes old slash commands and duplicate skills. Files you've changed in the old locations are kept and listed.
  • The delivery setting is no longer needed. Existing configs keep working.
  • Amazon Q Developer support is removed because it can't load skills. We recommend Kiro instead.

How to upgrade

Update the OpenSpec CLI, then run this in each project and commit the changes:

openspec update

To preview the changes first, run openspec update --dry-run.

Note: if you edited OpenSpec's skills directly in .agents/skills/, openspec update overwrites those edits. Save them before upgrading.

See the upgrade guide for more details.

Fixes

  • openspec completion install no longer edits PowerShell profiles when run from Windows cmd.exe.
  • openspec instructions apply no longer cuts off tasks that span several lines in tasks.md.

Full changelog: v1.17.0...v2.0.0

View release →

Graphify-Labs/graphify ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.107graphify 0.9.107 Stable2026-11-11T20:29:19Z
  • Fix: cpp a folded parameterless member declaration (bar()) records its implementation in definition_file as a path anchored to the same root as source_file, instead of a ../-walk relative to the cache/out root. The zero-argument reconciliation added in 0.9.106 stored the internal _cpp_source_key proof form, which _norm_source_file cannot re-anchor (it only relativizes absolute paths), so an out-of-tree corpus got a mangled definition_file. The sibling decl/def merge already stored the raw source_file for this reason; both paths now agree. The include-path proof still uses the key form.
  • Fix: llm a slice whose timeout or context overflow is recognised and given up on (it cannot be split further, or the retry depth is exhausted) now marks its file partial, so a document whose tail slice timed out is no longer cached and stamped complete from its other slices; it is re-queued on the next run while sibling files' results are kept (#5099, thanks @khrisshie).
  • Fix: php a route path scan stops at the closure's own argument, so $app->get('/users', function () { ... }) keeps GET /users instead of reading a trailing string argument after the closure as the route; a legitimate path before the closure is unchanged (#5135, thanks @mppyxx).
  • Fix: ids two module-level declarations of the same kind in one file whose names differ only by leading/trailing underscores (function _save(){} and function save(){ return _save() }) are two nodes again instead of one, and the call between them is a real save -> _save edge instead of a fabricated save -> save self-call; the declaration that appears first keeps the plain id and the other is salted. Affects JS, TS, C, C++, PHP, Ruby, Kotlin, Swift and Lua (#2810, thanks @khrisshie).
  • Fix: php methods of one class, trait, interface or enum whose names differ only by a leading/trailing _ (_x() / x()) are separate nodes instead of one swallowing the other: the one public name keeps its id and the others are salted, as in Python, C#, JS/TS and Solidity, so $this->x() and $b->x() reach x() instead of being lost and $this->_x() no longer looks like a self-call (#5137, thanks @khrisshie).
  • Fix: java members of one class whose names differ only by a leading/trailing _ (_name / name) or only by case (run / Run, which Java treats as distinct) get separate node ids instead of one swallowing the other; a public name keeps its id and the twins are salted, as in the other languages, so calls to each resolve correctly (#5151, thanks @rohit-jsfreaky).
  • Fix: test the copied-cache terraform id test accepts a worker pool that could not start under resource pressure (it falls back to sequential and warns), instead of asserting the pool always starts; a genuinely broken pool still fails the test (#5136, thanks @mppyxx).
  • Fix: php/js a call through an ESM namespace member (import * as ns; ns.handler()) or whole-module require resolves only when the target is actually callable, so a namespace member that names a non-callable export (an object literal, a constant) no longer gets a spurious call edge (#5144, thanks @ayushcodes10).
  • Perf: query the read path (query/explain/path/affected and the MCP graph load) no longer imports every language extractor, cutting import cost on read-only commands; graph output is unchanged (thanks @rohit-jsfreaky).
  • Perf: extract the AST worker pool is sized by the amount of uncached work (one worker per ~500 KB of uncached source, capped at CPU count and file count) when no worker count is set, so small incremental builds stop spinning up a full pool; output is invariant to worker count (thanks @rohit-jsfreaky).
  • Change: cloud the OSS surfaces (README, CLI help/install text, the translated docs in all 31 locales, and the agent skill) now point at the free plan with no card at app.graphify.com/signup, instead of a "14-day trial" at /login (the trial is paid-plans-only and needs a card, so the old copy conflated the two). Links carry utm_campaign=oss UTMs tagged per surface, so a signup can be attributed to where it came from.
  • Change: cloud the post-build CLI nudge is contextual. It is TTY-only (never in CI or the agent pipeline), respects GRAPHIFY_NO_TIPS/GRAPHIFY_NO_CTA, and shows on builds 1, 5 and 20 then stays quiet for good. When a local graph reaches 25k nodes, the free plan's per-repo cut-off, the nudge switches to a scale-specific line (tagged utm_content=scale) that sells Graphify Pro and links to pricing with the 14-day trial, instead of the free signup: a repo that size no longer fits the free plan, so pointing it at the free plan would land straight on the limit. The AST cache schema is bumped to 44; warm caches re-run the affected C++, PHP, Java and module-level-id extractions.

This week's release includes a Git Panel branch mode that lists every file changed since a base branch, a bookmark picker, configurable Git date formats, support for Claude Fable 5.2 for Anthropic BYOK users (plus Claude Mythos 5.2 if your organization has access), and a setting to turn off automatic agent thread titles.

Features

AI

Choose "Compare Against Base" from the new menu at the top of the Changes tab to pick the base branch, or run git panel: show branch changes to compare against the default branch. Files in this mode can be opened as diffs but not staged or restored. (#66482)

(#66533; thanks scarf005)

roboflow/supervision ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
0.31.7supervision-0.31.7 Stable2026-11-11T18:57:37Z

v0.31.7: pycocotools-matching mAP, stricter key point and CSV inputs, and edge-case fixes for zones and dataset exports

A fixes-only patch: mAP now matches pycocotools more closely, and several zone, key point and export edge cases stop failing.

  • MeanAveragePrecision uses pycocotools size buckets and the COCO crowd IoU for oriented-box crowd targets.
  • PolygonZone keeps its full mask when polygon coordinates reach their integer limit.
  • Key point selection and CSVSink now raise on inputs they used to silently misread.
  • DetectionDataset exports keep box area for empty COCO masks and, with simplification on, bridge more mask holes.
  • The OpenCV-free contourArea and convertScaleAbs fallbacks agree with OpenCV in more cases.

Drop-in upgrade, but check the Migration guide if you compare mAP values, pass extra CSV fields or select key points with boolean masks.

✨ Spotlights / highlights

MeanAveragePrecision matches pycocotools more closely

sv.metrics.MeanAveragePrecision now puts objects in the small, medium and large buckets that pycocotools gives them: a prediction's own geometry (box, mask or oriented-box polygon) decides, and its data["area"] is ignored. Target data["area"] is read as float64, so an area such as 9216.0004 is no longer scored as both medium and large. With MetricTarget.ORIENTED_BOUNDING_BOXES, targets with data["iscrowd"] set use the COCO crowd IoU, so a prediction inside a crowd region is ignored instead of counted as a false positive. (#2939, #2941)

import numpy as np
import supervision as sv
from supervision.metrics import MeanAveragePrecision, MetricTarget

# A small target with a perfect prediction, plus a higher-scored medium false positive
# whose area metadata claims it is small.
masks = np.zeros((2, 128, 128), dtype=bool)
masks[0, 0:20, 0:20] = True
masks[1, 60:100, 60:100] = True
predictions = sv.Detections(
    xyxy=np.array([[0, 0, 20, 20], [60, 60, 100, 100]], dtype=float),
    mask=masks,
    class_id=np.array([0, 0]),
    confidence=np.array([0.5, 0.9]),
    data={"area": np.array([400.0, 300.0])},
)
targets = sv.Detections(
    xyxy=np.array([[0, 0, 20, 20]], dtype=float),
    mask=masks[:1],
    class_id=np.array([0]),
)
result = (
    MeanAveragePrecision(metric_target=MetricTarget.MASKS)
    .update(predictions, targets)
    .compute()
)
result.small_objects.map50_95  # 1.0: the medium false positive no longer counts as small

Misread inputs raise, and scalar key point indexing works

sv.CSVSink raises ValueError when additional data uses a built-in column name such as confidence or x_min, instead of writing a duplicate column that shadows the detection value. sv.KeyPoints selection rejects a nonempty one-dimensional boolean mask whose length does not match the number of skeletons or keypoints, instead of silently selecting a subset, and a zero-dimensional NumPy integer row index combined with a keypoint list or mask now works. (#2934, #2943, #2930)

import numpy as np
import supervision as sv

detections = sv.Detections(
    xyxy=np.array([[0.0, 0.0, 10.0, 10.0]]), confidence=np.array([0.9])
)
with sv.CSVSink("detections.csv") as sink:
    sink.append(
        detections, {"confidence": 0.1}
    )  # ValueError: Additional data uses reserved CSV column names confidence.

PolygonZone survives integer coordinate limits

sv.PolygonZone computes its mask bounds as Python integers, so a polygon whose coordinates reach their dtype limit, for example uint8 255, keeps its full mask instead of overflowing, raising an error or silently creating a smaller zone. (#2945)

import numpy as np
import supervision as sv

polygon = np.array([[0, 0], [255, 0], [255, 3], [0, 3]], dtype=np.uint8)
sv.PolygonZone(polygon).mask.shape  # (5, 257)

The OpenCV-free fallbacks agree with OpenCV in more cases

The contourArea fallback adds the shoelace terms in OpenCV's order and reads integer points as float32, so sv.filter_polygons_by_area no longer flips at a min_area or max_area boundary between backends. The convertScaleAbs fallback scales in float32 with a single-rounding multiply-add, so values on a .5 tie such as 95 * 1.3 round to the same uint8 as OpenCV. (#2936, #2935)

Labels and dataset exports

sv.LabelAnnotator and sv.RichLabelAnnotator with smart_position=True keep an oversized label at the frame's left and top edges, and shaped right-to-left text in RichLabelAnnotator keeps its leading characters at the visible right edge. DetectionDataset.as_yolo and as_pascal_voc retry a failed mask-hole seam search with traced contours, so more masks export when you simplify them with approximation_percentage, and as_coco uses box area for empty mask placeholders that have no finite stored area. (#2940, #2933, #2942)

🔄 Migration guide

Nothing was removed, renamed or deprecated, but a few fixes change what existing code returns or accepts:

  • Different values: MeanAveragePrecision sizes a prediction by its geometry and ignores its data["area"], and reads target data["area"] as float64, so small, medium and large mAP can move. (#2939) Oriented-box mAP on iscrowd targets can rise, never fall, because crowd overlaps are ignored. (#2941) as_coco writes box area, not 0, for empty mask placeholders. (#2942) Oversized labels with smart_position=True stop at the left and top frame edges. (#2940)
  • New errors: CSVSink raises ValueError for extra fields named like a built-in column. (#2934) KeyPoints selection raises IndexError for a nonempty boolean mask of the wrong length. (#2943)
  • Without OpenCV: the contourArea fallback can flip filter_polygons_by_area at a boundary (#2936), and the convertScaleAbs fallback rounds some uint8 results one level differently, up or down (#2935). Regenerate stored references made with the NumPy fallback.

📝 Notable changes

🔧 Fixed

Metrics

  • MeanAveragePrecision puts objects in the small, medium and large buckets pycocotools gives them: prediction area comes from its own geometry (box, mask or oriented-box polygon), and target data["area"] is read as float64. (#2939)
  • MeanAveragePrecision with MetricTarget.ORIENTED_BOUNDING_BOXES uses the COCO crowd IoU for targets with data["iscrowd"] set, so predictions inside a crowd region are ignored instead of counted as false positives. (#2941)

Key points, zones and sinks

  • PolygonZone preserves mask dimensions when integer polygon coordinates reach their dtype limit, instead of overflowing. (#2945)
  • KeyPoints rejects nonempty one-dimensional boolean selection masks whose length does not match the number of skeletons or keypoints; keypoint selection on KeyPoints.empty() stays empty. (#2943)
  • KeyPoints accepts a zero-dimensional NumPy integer row index combined with a list or boolean mask of keypoints, preserving coordinates and aligned fields instead of raising ValueError. (#2930)
  • CSVSink raises ValueError when additional data uses a built-in column name such as confidence or x_min, instead of writing duplicate columns. (#2934)

Annotators and the NumPy fallback without OpenCV

  • LabelAnnotator and RichLabelAnnotator with smart_position=True keep an oversized label at the frame's left and top edges; shaped right-to-left text in RichLabelAnnotator retains its leading characters at the visible right edge. (#2940)
  • The contourArea fallback returns exactly what cv2.contourArea returns, so filter_polygons_by_area agrees across backends at its boundaries. (#2936)
  • The convertScaleAbs fallback scales in float32 with a single-rounding multiply-add, like OpenCV's fused float32 path. (#2935)

Dataset exports

  • DetectionDataset.as_yolo and as_pascal_voc retry a failed mask-hole seam search with traced contours when simplification removes usable vertices; as_yolo and as_pascal_voc still raise if no seam is found, and as_coco also tries this for iscrowd=0 annotations. (#2933)
  • DetectionDataset.as_coco uses bounding-box area for empty mask placeholders when no finite stored area is available and no segmentation is exported. (#2942)

📚 Docs

  • The "Edit this page" links now point at the develop branch. (#2944)
  • The SAHI notebook shows the corrected tile size output. (#2937)
  • The SAHI notebook calls InferenceSlicer with overlap_wh and no longer passes the removed overlap_ratio_wh, so its cells run again, and outputs from the old 2560-pixel image were cleared. (#2938)
  • The CSVSink how-to now says that extra fields named like a built-in column raise ValueError, and the snap_boxes and MeanAveragePrecisionResult docstrings describe oversized boxes and the inclusive size limits.

🏆 Contributors

Welcome to our first-time contributors Pranjal Mishra, Chris Censullo and viwe-monai!

  • Pranjal Mishra (@pmxlr8, LinkedIn) — matched MeanAveragePrecision size buckets and oriented-box crowd IoU to pycocotools.
  • Mohammad Sohail Shaikh (@sohailshk, LinkedIn) — kept oversized labels at the frame's left edge and bridged mask holes in dataset exports.
  • Vlad Voropaev (@voropaevv, LinkedIn) — rejected CSVSink extra fields that shadow built-in columns and kept box area for empty COCO mask placeholders.
  • Ahmet Mert Özacar (@AhmetMert01) — validated boolean key point selection masks and accepted scalar NumPy row indices.
  • A Aswanth Raj (@aswanth-07, LinkedIn) — kept PolygonZone masks intact at integer coordinate limits.
  • kevin (@kevin9327) — made the OpenCV-free contourArea and convertScaleAbs fallbacks match OpenCV.
  • Chris Censullo (@censullo) — corrected the tile size output in the SAHI notebook.
  • viwe-monai (@viwe-monai) — pointed the "Edit this page" links at the develop branch.
  • Jirka Borovec (@Borda, LinkedIn) — updated dependency pins and prepared the release.

Automated contributions: pre-commit.ci, claude, Codex

Full changelog: https://github.com/roboflow/supervision/compare/0.31.6...0.31.7 Co-authored with /oss:release.

coreyhaines31/marketingskills ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v2.14.12v2.14.12 — Skills install cleanly under regex skill scanners that block on prompt-injection phrases. Stable2026-11-11T17:49:23Z

Skills install cleanly under regex skill scanners that block on prompt-injection phrases.

  • ads (2.7.4 → 2.7.5): the untrusted-data rule in references/audit-guardrails.md describes an embedded "drop your earlier instructions" directive instead of quoting the literal injection phrase, which Hermes' Skills Guard flagged as a critical prompt injection and blocked on install. Same rule, same meaning.
  • lead-magnets (2.1.4 → 2.1.5): references/content-to-skill-corpus-design.md and eval 9 describe the injected transcript line instead of quoting it, for the same reason. The eval still tests that transcript text is treated as data.
  • schema: eval 8's expected output rewords "do not ... tell the user to delete" so it no longer reads as an instruction to hide information from the user. Evals only, no version bump.

Skill ZIP assets

Complete source catalog: 53 skills. Claude web target: 53 skills with reviewed short descriptions. Perplexity Computer target: 53 skills.

Choose <name>-claude-web.zip for the documented single-folder web upload format. Choose <name>-perplexity.zip for Perplexity Computer (SKILL.md at the archive root). Source ZIPs retain original metadata; they are not substitutes for omitted web ZIPs. Live client upload, account limits, and invocation behavior have not been verified.

v2.14.11v2.14.11 — directory-submissions (2.1.7 → 2.2.0): a listing kit (references/listing-kit.md) to fill… Stable2026-11-11T17:44:52Z
  • directory-submissions (2.1.7 → 2.2.0): a listing kit (references/listing-kit.md) to fill in once before the first submission: every fact directories ask for, the copy at each common length limit (40, 60, 100, 160, and ~500 characters), asset sizes, and a submission log with statuses for skips and hand-offs. A new section covers agent-run submissions: check the log first, use a documented API or /llms.txt route before a form, open embedded forms on their own page, fill only visible fields and stop at any CAPTCHA or login, keep personal phone numbers and addresses off listings, no paid listings without approval, close email verification loops, and read an awesome list's contributing rules and recent merges before opening a PR. Directories without an API or PR route are filled with browser or computer use: the user signs in once per batch, the agent maps fields to the kit, uploads assets, shows a screenshot of the filled form for approval, submits, and logs the confirmation; CAPTCHAs, logins, payments, and terms go back to the user and the agent resumes after. A new references/agent-registry-files.md lists the repo files agent registries read (server.json, glama.json, gemini-extension.json, Cursor and Claude plugin manifests, SKILL.md), checked against each registry's docs: only the Gemini CLI gallery lists a repo from the file alone; skills.sh lists from install counts; the rest need a claim or a reviewed submission. Prompted by Ilias Ism's directory-submissions skill.
